tool_result Bash
```
usage: desloppify [-h] [--lang LANG] [--exclude PATTERN]
                  {scan,status,tree,show,next,resolve,ignore,fix,plan,viz,detect,move,review,zone,config,dev,langs,update-skill}
                  ...

Desloppify — codebase health tracker

positional arguments:
  {scan,status,tree,show,next,resolve,ignore,fix,plan,viz,detect,move,review,zone,config,dev,langs,update-skill}
    scan                Run all detectors, update state, show diff
    status              Score dashboard with per-tier progress
    tree                Annotated codebase tree (text)
    show                Dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or ID
    next                Show next highest-priority open finding
    resolve             Mark finding(s) as fixed/wontfix/false_positive/open
    ignore              Add pattern to ignore list, remove matching findings
    fix                 Auto-fix mechanical issues
    plan                Generate prioritized markdown plan from state
    viz                 Generate interactive HTML treemap
    detect              Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    move                Move a file or directory and update all import
                        references
    review              Prepare or import holistic subjective review
    zone                Show/set/clear zone classifications
    config              Show/set/unset project configuration
    dev                 Developer utilities
    langs               List all available language plugins with depth and
                        tools
    update-skill        Install or update the desloppify skill/agent document

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --lang LANG           Language to scan (bash, clojure, csharp, cxx, dart,
                        elixir, erlang, fsharp, gdscript, go, haskell, java,
                        javascript, kotlin, lua, nim, ocaml, perl, php,
                        powershell, python, r, ruby, rust, scala, swift,
                        typescript, zig). Auto-detected if omitted.
  --exclude PATTERN     Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match;
                        repeatable)

workflow:
  scan                          Run all detectors, update state, show diff
  status                        Score dashboard with per-tier progress
  tree                          Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
  show <pattern>                Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
  resolve <pattern> <status>    Mark findings as open/fixed/wontfix/false_positive
  ignore <pattern>              Suppress findings matching a pattern
  zone show                     Show zone classifications for all files
  zone set <file> <zone>        Override zone for a file
  review --prepare              Prepare holistic codebase review data
  review --import FILE          Import review findings from JSON
  review --validate-import FILE Validate import payload/trust mode without mutating state
  review --external-start       Start external blind review session (Claude cloud)
  review --external-submit      Submit external session results with canonical provenance
  review --merge                Merge duplicate review findings
  plan                          Generate prioritized markdown plan

examples:
  desloppify scan --skip-slow
  desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
  desloppify tree --focus shared/components --sort findings --depth 3
  desloppify tree --detail --focus shared/components/MediaLightbox --min-loc 300
  desloppify show src/shared/components/PromptEditorModal.tsx
  desloppify show gods
  desloppify show "src/shared/components/MediaLightbox"
  desloppify resolve fixed "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" --note "removed unused React import" --attest "I have actually removed the unused React import from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify resolve fixed "logs::src/foo.tsx::*" --note "removed debug logs" --attest "I have actually removed all console.log calls from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify resolve wontfix deprecated --note "migration in progress" --attest "I have actually verified these are tracked in the migration plan and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify ignore "smells::*::async_no_await" --attest "I have actually verified these are intentional fire-and-forget patterns and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify detect logs --top 10
  desloppify detect dupes --threshold 0.9
  desloppify review --validate-import findings.json --attested-external --attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness of overall score and is unbiased."
  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude
  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <session_id> --import findings.json
  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --retrospective
  desloppify dev scaffold-lang go --extension .go --marker go.mod --default-src .
  desloppify move src/shared/hooks/useFoo.ts src/shared/hooks/video/useFoo.ts --dry-run
  desloppify move scripts/foo/bar.py scripts/foo/baz/bar.py
```

```
    97→        provenance = {}
    98→    elif not isinstance(provenance, dict):
    99→        errors.append("provenance must be an object when provided")
   100→        provenance = {}
   101→
   102→    dimension_notes = payload.get("dimension_notes")
   103→    if dimension_notes is None:
   104→        dimension_notes = {}
   105→    elif not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   106→        errors.append("dimension_notes must be an object when provided")
   107→        dimension_notes = {}
   108→
   109→    policy = payload.get(_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY)
   110→    normalized_policy = (
   111→        policy if isinstance(policy, dict) else AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   112→    )
   113→    if errors:
   114→        return None, errors
   115→    return (
   116→        {
   117→            "findings": findings,
   118→            "assessments": assessments,
   119→            "reviewed_files": normalized_reviewed_files,
   120→            "review_scope": review_scope,
   121→            "provenance": provenance,
   122→            "dimension_notes": dimension_notes,
   123→            [REDACTED],
   124→        },
   125→        [],
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→def _default_blind_packet_path() -> Path:
   130→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
   131→
   132→
   133→def _is_sha256_hex(raw: object) -> bool:
   134→    return (
   135→        isinstance(raw, str)
   136→        and len(raw) == 64
   137→        and all(ch in "0123456789abcdefABCDEF" for ch in raw)
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def _hash_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str | None:
   142→    try:
   143→        data = path.read_bytes()
   144→    except OSError:
   145→        return None
   146→    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   147→
   148→
   149→def _resolve_packet_path(raw_path: object) -> Path | None:
   150→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str):
   151→        return None
   152→    text = raw_path.strip()
   153→    if not text:
   154→        return None
   155→    path = Path(text)
   156→    return path if path.is_absolute() else get_project_root() / path
   157→
   158→
   159→def _assessment_provenance_status(
   160→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   161→    *,
   162→    import_file: str,
   163→) -> AssessmentProvenanceModel:
   164→    """Evaluate whether assessments come from a trusted blind batch artifact."""
   165→    provenance = findings_data["provenance"]
   166→    if not provenance:
   167→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   168→            trusted=False,
   169→            reason="missing provenance metadata",
   170→            import_file=import_file,
   171→        )
   172→
   173→    kind = str(provenance.get("kind", "")).strip()
   174→    if kind != _BLIND_PROVENANCE_KIND:
   175→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   176→            trusted=False,
   177→            reason=f"unsupported provenance kind: {kind or '<missing>'}",
   178→            import_file=import_file,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    if provenance.get("blind") is not True:
   182→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   183→            trusted=False,
   184→            reason="provenance is not marked blind=true",
   185→            import_file=import_file,
   186→        )
   187→
   188→    runner = str(provenance.get("runner", "")).strip().lower()
   189→    if runner not in _SUPPORTED_BLIND_REVIEW_RUNNERS:
   190→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   191→            trusted=False,
   192→            reason=f"unsupported runner in provenance: {runner or '<missing>'}",
   193→            import_file=import_file,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→    packet_hash = provenance.get("packet_sha256")
   197→    if not _is_sha256_hex(packet_hash):
   198→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   199→            trusted=False,
   200→            reason="missing or invalid packet_sha256 in provenance",
   201→            import_file=import_file,
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    packet_path = _resolve_packet_path(provenance.get("packet_path"))
   205→    if packet_path is None:
   206→        packet_path = _default_blind_packet_path()
   207→    if not packet_path.exists():
   208→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   209→            trusted=False,
   210→            reason=f"blind packet not found: {packet_path}",
   211→            import_file=import_file,
   212→        )
   213→    observed_hash = _hash_file_sha256(packet_path)
   214→    if observed_hash is None:
   215→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   216→            trusted=False,
   217→            reason=f"unable to hash blind packet: {packet_path}",
   218→            import_file=import_file,
   219→        )
   220→    if observed_hash != packet_hash:
   221→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   222→            trusted=False,
   223→            reason=(
   224→                "blind packet hash mismatch "
   225→                f"(expected {packet_hash[:12]}..., got {observed_hash[:12]}...)"
   226→            ),
   227→            import_file=import_file,
   228→        )
   229→
   230→    return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   231→        trusted=True,
   232→        reason="trusted blind subagent provenance",
   233→        runner=runner,
   234→        packet_path=str(packet_path),
   235→        packet_sha256=packet_hash,
   236→        import_file=import_file,
   237→    )
   238→
   239→
   240→def resolve_override_context(
   241→    *,
   242→    manual_override: bool,
   243→    manual_attest: str | None,
   244→    assessment_override: bool,
   245→    assessment_note: str | None,
   246→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   247→    """Support legacy assessment_* flags while preferring manual_* naming."""
   248→    override = bool(manual_override or assessment_override)
   249→    attest = (
   250→        manual_attest
   251→        if isinstance(manual_attest, str) and manual_attest.strip()
   252→        else assessment_note
   253→    )
   254→    if isinstance(attest, str):
   255→        attest = attest.strip()
   256→    return override, attest
   257→
   258→
   259→def _validate_attested_external_attestation(attest: str | None) -> str | None:
   260→    """Validate and normalize attestation text for attested external imports."""
   261→    if not isinstance(attest, str) or not attest.strip():
   262→        return None
   263→    text = attest.strip()
   264→    lowered = text.lower()
   265→    if all(phrase in lowered for phrase in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_REQUIRED_PHRASES):
   266→        return text
   267→    return None
   268→
   269→
   270→def _print_import_error_hints(
   271→    errors: list[str],
   272→    *,
   273→    import_file: str,
   274→    colorize_fn,
   275→) -> None:
   276→    """Print actionable retry commands for common import policy failures."""
   277→    joined = " ".join(err.lower() for err in errors)
   278→    quoted_import = shlex.quote(import_file)
   279→    import_cmd = (
   280→        "desloppify review --import "
   281→        f"{quoted_import} --attested-external --attest "
   282→        f"\"{_ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_ATTEST_EXAMPLE}\""
   283→    )
   284→    validate_cmd = (
   285→        "desloppify review --validate-import "
   286→        f"{quoted_import} --attested-external --attest "
   287→        f"\"{_ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_ATTEST_EXAMPLE}\""
   288→    )
   289→    findings_only_cmd = f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import}"
   290→
   291→    if "--attested-external requires --attest containing both" in joined:
   292→        print(
   293→            colorize_fn(
   294→                "  Hint: rerun with the required attestation template:",
   295→                "yellow",
   296→            ),
   297→            file=sys.stderr,
   298→        )
   299→        print(colorize_fn(f"    `{import_cmd}`", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   300→        print(
   301→            colorize_fn(
   302→                f"  Preflight without state changes: `{validate_cmd}`",
   303→                "dim",
   304→            ),
   305→            file=sys.stderr,
   306→        )
   307→        return
   308→
   309→    if (
   310→        "--attested-external requires valid blind packet provenance" in joined
   311→        or "supports runner='claude'" in joined
   312→    ):
   313→        print(
   314→            colorize_fn(
   315→                "  Hint: if provenance is valid, rerun with:",
   316→                "yellow",
   317→            ),
   318→            file=sys.stderr,
   319→        )
   320→        print(colorize_fn(f"    `{import_cmd}`", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   321→        print(
   322→            colorize_fn(
   323→                f"  Preflight without state changes: `{validate_cmd}`",
   324→                "dim",
   325→            ),
   326→            file=sys.stderr,
   327→        )
   328→        print(
   329→            colorize_fn(
   330→                f"  Findings-only fallback: `{findings_only_cmd}`",
   331→                "dim",
   332→            ),
   333→            file=sys.stderr,
   334→        )
   335→
   336→
   337→def _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   338→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   339→    *,
   340→    import_file: str,
   341→    attested_external: bool,
   342→    attested_attest: str | None,
   343→    manual_override: bool,
   344→    manual_attest: str | None,
   345→    trusted_assessment_source: bool,
   346→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None,
   347→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   348→    """Apply trust gating for assessment imports (findings import always allowed)."""
   349→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   350→    has_assessments = bool(assessments)
   351→    assessment_count = len(assessments) if has_assessments else 0
   352→    provenance_status = _assessment_provenance_status(
   353→        findings_data, import_file=import_file
   354→    )
   355→    policy = AssessmentImportPolicyModel(
   356→        assessments_present=has_assessments,
   357→        assessment_count=int(assessment_count),
   358→        trusted=False,
   359→        mode="none",
   360→        reason="",
   361→        provenance=provenance_status,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→    def _attach_policy(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   365→        normalized = dict(payload)
   366→        normalized[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   367→        return normalized
   368→
   369→    if not has_assessments:
   370→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   371→
   372→    if trusted_assessment_source:
   373→        policy = replace(
   374→            policy,
   375→            mode="trusted_internal",
   376→            trusted=True,
   377→            reason=(trusted_assessment_label or "trusted internal run-batches import"),
   378→        )
   379→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   380→
   381→    if attested_external:
   382→        normalized_attest = _validate_attested_external_attestation(attested_attest)
   383→        if normalized_attest is None:
   384→            return None, [
   385→                "--attested-external requires --attest containing both "
   386→                "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'"
   387→            ]
   388→        if provenance_status.trusted is not True:
   389→            return None, [
   390→                "--attested-external requires valid blind packet provenance "
   391→                f"(current status: {provenance_status.reason or 'untrusted provenance'})"
   392→            ]
   393→        runner = provenance_status.runner.strip().lower()
   394→        if runner not in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_RUNNERS:
   395→            return None, [
   396→                "--attested-external currently supports runner='claude' provenance only"
   397→            ]
   398→        policy = replace(
   399→            policy,
   400→            mode="attested_external",
   401→            trusted=True,
   402→            reason="attested external blind subagent provenance",
   403→            attest=normalized_attest,
   404→        )
   405→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   406→
   407→    if manual_override:
   408→        if not isinstance(manual_attest, str) or not manual_attest.strip():
   409→            return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   410→        policy = replace(
   411→            policy,
   412→            mode="manual_override",
   413→            reason="manual override attested by operator",
   414→            attest=manual_attest.strip(),
   415→        )
   416→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   417→
   418→    policy = replace(policy, mode="findings_only")
   419→    if findings_data["provenance"]:
   420→        provenance_reason = provenance_status.reason.strip()
   421→        if provenance_status.trusted is True:
   422→            policy = replace(
   423→                policy,
   424→                reason=(
   425→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust even when provenance appears valid; "
   426→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   427→                ),
   428→            )
   429→        elif provenance_reason:
   430→            policy = replace(
   431→                policy,
   432→                reason=(
   433→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust "
   434→                    f"({provenance_reason}); run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   435→                ),
   436→            )
   437→        else:
   438→            policy = replace(
   439→                policy,
   440→                reason=(
   441→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust; "
   442→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   443→                ),
   444→            )
   445→    else:
   446→        policy = replace(
   447→            policy,
   448→            reason="missing trusted run-batches source; imported findings only",
   449→        )
   450→    payload = dict(findings_data)
   451→    payload["assessments"] = {}
   452→    payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   453→    return payload, []
   454→
   455→
   456→def _has_non_empty_strings(items: object) -> bool:
   457→    """Return True when ``items`` is a list with at least one non-empty string."""
   458→    return isinstance(items, list) and any(
   459→        isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in items
   460→    )
   461→
   462→
   463→def _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   464→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   465→    *,
   466→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   467→) -> list[str]:
   468→    """Validate strict holistic finding schema expected by issue import."""
   469→    findings = findings_data["findings"]
   470→
   471→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   472→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip():
   473→        _, dimension_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   474→        allowed_dimensions = set(dimension_prompts)
   475→
   476→    errors: list[str] = []
   477→    for idx, entry in enumerate(findings):
   478→        _normalized: ReviewFindingPayload | None
   479→        _normalized, entry_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
   480→            entry,
   481→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
   482→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions or None,
   483→            allow_dismissed=True,
   484→        )
   485→        for message in entry_errors:
   486→            if (
   487→                "is not allowed" in message
   488→                and lang_name
   489→                and "dimension '" in message
   490→            ):
   491→                message = message.replace(
   492→                    "is not allowed",
   493→                    f"is not valid for language '{lang_name}'",
   494→                )
   495→            errors.append(message)
   496→    return errors
   497→
   498→
   499→def _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings: object) -> set[str]:
   500→    """Return dimensions with explicit improvement guidance in findings payload."""
   501→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   502→        return set()
   503→    dims: set[str] = set()
   504→    for entry in findings:
   505→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   506→            continue
   507→        dim = entry.get("dimension")
   508→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   509→            continue
   510→        suggestion = entry.get("suggestion")
   511→        if isinstance(suggestion, str) and suggestion.strip():
   512→            dims.add(dim.strip())
   513→    return dims
   514→
   515→
   516→def _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(dimension_notes: object) -> set[str]:
   517→    """Return dimensions with concrete review evidence in dimension_notes payload."""
   518→    if not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   519→        return set()
   520→    dims: set[str] = set()
   521→    for dim, note in dimension_notes.items():
   522→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   523→            continue
   524→        if not isinstance(note, dict):
   525→            continue
   526→        if not _has_non_empty_strings(note.get("evidence")):
   527→            continue
   528→        dims.add(dim.strip())
   529→    return dims
   530→
   531→
   532→def _validate_assessment_feedback(
   533→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   534→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   535→    """Return dimensions missing required feedback and required low-score findings."""
   536→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   537→    if not assessments:
   538→        return [], []
   539→
   540→    finding_dims = _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings_data["findings"])
   541→    feedback_dims = set(finding_dims)
   542→    feedback_dims.update(
   543→        _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(findings_data["dimension_notes"])
   544→    )
   545→    missing_feedback: list[str] = []
   546→    missing_low_score_findings: list[str] = []
   547→    for dim_name, payload in assessments.items():
   548→        if not isinstance(dim_name, str) or not dim_name.strip():
   549→            continue
   550→        score = coerce_assessment_score(payload)
   551→        if score is None:
   552→            continue
   553→        if score_requires_dimension_finding(score) and dim_name not in finding_dims:
   554→            missing_low_score_findings.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   555→        if score_requires_explicit_feedback(score) and dim_name not in feedback_dims:
   556→            missing_feedback.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   557→    return sorted(missing_feedback), sorted(missing_low_score_findings)
   558→
   559→
   560→def _parse_and_validate_import(
   561→    import_file: str,
   562→    *,
   563→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   564→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   565→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   566→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   567→    attested_external: bool = False,
   568→    manual_override: bool = False,
   569→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   570→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   571→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   572→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   573→    """Parse and validate a review import file (pure function).
   574→
   575→    Returns ``(data, errors)`` where *data* is the normalized payload on
   576→    success, or ``None`` when errors prevent import.
   577→    """
   578→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   579→    if not findings_path.exists():
   580→        return None, [f"file not found: {import_file}"]
   581→    try:
   582→        findings_data = json.loads(findings_path.read_text())
   583→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   584→        return None, [f"error reading findings: {exc}"]
   585→
   586→    if isinstance(findings_data, list):
   587→        findings_data = {"findings": findings_data}
   588→
   589→    if not isinstance(findings_data, dict):
   590→        return None, ["findings file must contain a JSON array or object"]
   591→
   592→    if "findings" not in findings_data:
   593→        return None, ["findings object must contain a 'findings' key"]
   594→    normalized_findings_data, shape_errors = _normalize_import_payload_shape(
   595→        findings_data
   596→    )
   597→    if shape_errors:
   598→        return None, shape_errors
   599→    assert normalized_findings_data is not None
   600→
   601→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   602→        manual_override=manual_override,
   603→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   604→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   605→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   606→    )
   607→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
   608→        return None, [
   609→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
   610→        ]
   611→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
   612→        return None, [
   613→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   614→            "attested score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   615→        ]
   616→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
   617→        return None, [
   618→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   619→            "manual score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   620→        ]
   621→    findings_data, policy_errors = _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   622→        normalized_findings_data,
   623→        import_file=import_file,
   624→        attested_external=attested_external,
   625→        attested_attest=override_attest,
   626→        manual_override=override_enabled,
   627→        manual_attest=override_attest,
   628→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   629→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   630→    )
   631→    if policy_errors:
   632→        return None, policy_errors
   633→    assert findings_data is not None
   634→
   635→    missing_feedback, missing_low_score_findings = _validate_assessment_feedback(
   636→        findings_data
   637→    )
   638→    if missing_low_score_findings:
   639→        if override_enabled:
   640→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   641→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   642→            return findings_data, []
   643→        return None, [
   644→            f"assessments below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include at "
   645→            "least one finding for that same dimension with a concrete suggestion. "
   646→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_low_score_findings)}"
   647→        ]
   648→
   649→    if missing_feedback:
   650→        if override_enabled:
   651→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   652→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   653→            return findings_data, []
   654→        return None, [
   655→            f"assessments below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include explicit feedback "
   656→            "(finding with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, or "
   657→            "dimension_notes evidence for that dimension). "
   658→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_feedback)}"
   659→        ]
   660→
   661→    schema_errors = _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   662→        findings_data,
   663→        lang_name=lang_name,
   664→    )
   665→    if schema_errors and not allow_partial:
   666→        visible_errors = schema_errors[:10]
   667→        remaining = len(schema_errors) - len(visible_errors)
   668→        errors = [
   669→            "findings schema validation failed for holistic import. "
   670→            "Fix payload or rerun with --allow-partial to continue."
   671→        ]
   672→        errors.extend(visible_errors)
   673→        if remaining > 0:
   674→            errors.append(f"... {remaining} additional schema error(s) omitted")
   675→        return None, errors
   676→
   677→    return findings_data, []
   678→
   679→
   680→def load_import_findings_data(
   681→    import_file: str,
   682→    *,
   683→    colorize_fn=None,
   684→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   685→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   686→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   687→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   688→    attested_external: bool = False,
   689→    manual_override: bool = False,
   690→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   691→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   692→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   693→) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   694→    """Load and normalize review import payload to object format.
   695→
   696→    Raises ``ImportPayloadLoadError`` when validation fails.
   697→    """
   698→    data, errors = _parse_and_validate_import(
   699→        import_file,
   700→        lang_name=lang_name,
   701→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   702→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   703→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   704→        attested_external=attested_external,
   705→        manual_override=manual_override,
   706→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   707→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   708→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   709→    )
   710→    if errors:
   711→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(errors)
   712→    assert data is not None  # guaranteed when errors is empty
   713→    return data
   714→
   715→
   716→def print_import_load_errors(
   717→    errors: list[str],
   718→    *,
   719→    import_file: str,
   720→    colorize_fn,
   721→) -> None:
   722→    """Print import payload validation errors and actionable hints."""
   723→    for err in errors:
   724→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {err}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   725→    _print_import_error_hints(errors, import_file=import_file, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   726→
   727→
   728→def assessment_policy_from_payload(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> AssessmentImportPolicy:
   729→    """Return parsed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   730→    policy = payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY]
   731→    if isinstance(policy, dict):
   732→        return policy
   733→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   734→
   735→
   736→def assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   737→    payload: ReviewImportPayload,
   738→) -> AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
   739→    """Return typed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   740→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(assessment_policy_from_payload(payload))
   741→
   742→
   743→def assessment_mode_label(policy: AssessmentImportPolicy) -> str:
   744→    """Return a user-facing label for the selected assessment import mode."""
   745→    mode = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy).mode.strip().lower()
   746→    return _ASSESSMENT_MODE_LABELS.get(mode, f"unknown ({mode or 'none'})")
   747→
   748→
   749→def print_assessment_mode_banner(
   750→    policy: AssessmentImportPolicy,
   751→    *,
   752→    colorize_fn,
   753→) -> None:
   754→    """Print the selected assessment import mode to make policy explicit."""
   755→    policy_model = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy)
   756→    mode = policy_model.mode.strip().lower()
   757→    assessments_present = bool(policy_model.assessments_present)
   758→    if not assessments_present and mode == "none":
   759→        return
   760→    style = "yellow" if mode in {"manual_override", "findings_only"} else "dim"
   761→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Assessment import mode: {assessment_mode_label(policy)}", style))
   762→
   763→
   764→def print_assessment_policy_notice(
   765→    policy: AssessmentImportPolicy,

   769→) -> None:
   770→    """Render trust/override status for assessment-bearing imports."""

   772→    if not policy_model.assessments_present:
   773→        return
   774→    mode = policy_model.mode.strip().lower()
   775→    reason = policy_model.reason.strip()
   776→
   777→    if mode == "trusted":
   778→        packet_path = policy_model.provenance.packet_path.strip() or None
   779→        detail = f" · blind packet {packet_path}" if packet_path else ""
   780→        print(

   786→        return

   788→    if mode == "trusted_internal":

   790→        reason_text = policy_model.reason.strip()
   791→        suffix = f" ({reason_text})" if reason_text else ""
   792→        print(

   797→        )
   798→        return
   799→
   800→    if mode == "manual_override":

   802→        print(

   805→                "yellow",
   806→            )
   807→        )
   808→        if reason:
   809→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   810→        return
   811→
   812→    if mode == "attested_external":
   813→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   814→        print(

   818→            )
   819→        )
   820→        if reason:

   822→        return
   823→

   826→        print(
   827→            colorize_fn(

   830→                "yellow",

   832→        )
   833→        if reason:

   847→        print(

   863→
   864→def print_skipped_validation_details(diff: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   865→    """Print validation warnings for skipped imported findings."""
   866→    n_skipped = diff.get("skipped", 0)
   867→    if n_skipped <= 0:
   868→        return
   869→    print(
   870→        colorize_fn(
   871→            f"\n  \u26a0 {n_skipped} finding(s) skipped (validation errors):",
   872→            "yellow",
   873→        )
   874→    )
   875→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   876→        reasons = detail["missing"]
   877→        missing_fields = [r for r in reasons if not r.startswith("invalid ")]
   878→        validation_errors = [r for r in reasons if r.startswith("invalid ")]
   879→        parts = []
   880→        if missing_fields:
   881→            parts.append(f"missing {', '.join(missing_fields)}")
   882→        parts.extend(validation_errors)
   883→        print(
   884→            colorize_fn(
   885→                f"    #{detail['index']} ({detail['identifier']}): {'; '.join(parts)}",
   886→                "yellow",
   887→            )
   888→        )
   889→
   890→
   891→def print_assessments_summary(state: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   892→    """Print holistic subjective assessment summary when present."""
   893→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   894→    if not assessments:
   895→        return
   896→    parts = [
   897→        f"{key.replace('_', ' ')} {value['score']}"
   898→        for key, value in sorted(assessments.items())
   899→    ]
   900→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Assessments: {', '.join(parts)}", "bold"))
   901→
   902→
   903→def print_open_review_summary(state: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> str:
   904→    """Print current open review finding count and return next command."""
   905→    open_review = [
   906→        finding
   907→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   908→        if finding["status"] == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   909→    ]
   910→    if not open_review:
   911→        return "desloppify scan"
   912→    print(
   913→        colorize_fn(
   914→            f"\n  {len(open_review)} review issue{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open total "
   915→            f"({len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open total)",
   916→            "bold",
   917→        )
   918→    )
   919→    print(colorize_fn("  Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue", "dim"))
   920→    return "desloppify show review --status open"
   921→
   922→
   923→def print_review_import_scores_and_integrity(
   924→    state: dict[str, Any],
   925→    config: dict[str, Any],
   926→    *,
   927→    state_mod,
   928→    target_strict_score_from_config_fn,
   929→    subjective_at_target_fn,
   930→    subjective_rerun_command_fn,
   931→    colorize_fn,
   932→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   933→    """Print score snapshot plus subjective integrity warnings."""
   934→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   935→    if scores.overall is not None and scores.objective is not None and scores.strict is not None:
   936→        print(
   937→            colorize_fn(
   938→                f"\n  Current scores: overall {scores.overall:.1f}/100 · "
   939→                f"objective {scores.objective:.1f}/100 · strict {scores.strict:.1f}/100",
   940→                "dim",
   941→            )
   942→        )
   943→
   944→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config_fn(config, fallback=95.0)
   945→    at_target = subjective_at_target_fn(
   946→        state,
   947→        state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   948→        target=target_strict,
   949→    )
   950→    if not at_target:
   951→        return []
   952→
   953→    command = subjective_rerun_command_fn(at_target, max_items=5)
   954→    count = len(at_target)
   955→    if count >= 2:
   956→        print(
   957→            colorize_fn(
   958→                "  WARNING: "
   959→                f"{count} subjective scores match the target score. "
   960→                "On the next scan, those dimensions will be reset to 0.0 by the anti-gaming safeguard "
   961→                f"unless you rerun and re-import objective reviews first: {command}",
   962→                "red",
   963→            )
   964→        )
   965→    else:
   966→        print(
   967→            colorize_fn(
   968→                "  WARNING: "
   969→                f"{count} subjective score matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   970→                f"Can you rerun it by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   971→                "yellow",
   972→            )
   973→        )
   974→    return at_target
   975→
   976→

   978→    "ImportPayloadLoadError",
   979→    "assessment_mode_label",
   980→    "assessment_policy_model_from_payload",
```

```
   143→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   144→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   145→    config: dict[str, object],
   146→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   147→    lang: LangRun | None,
   148→) -> LangRun | None:
   149→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   150→    if not lang:
   151→        return None
   152→
   153→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   154→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   155→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   156→        lang,
   157→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   158→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   159→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   160→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   161→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   162→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   163→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   164→        ),
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   168→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   169→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   170→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   171→    }
   172→    return runtime_lang
   173→
   174→
   175→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   176→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   177→    *,
   178→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   179→) -> int:
   180→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   181→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   182→
   183→    reset_keys = {
   184→        key.strip()
   185→        for key in assessments
   186→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   187→    }
   188→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   189→
   190→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   191→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   192→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   193→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   194→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   195→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   196→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   197→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   198→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   199→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   200→            payload.pop("components", None)
   201→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   202→            continue
   203→        assessments[key] = {
   204→            "score": 0.0,
   205→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   206→            "assessed_at": now,
   207→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   208→            "placeholder": True,
   209→        }
   210→    return len(reset_keys)
   211→
   212→
   213→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   214→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   215→) -> int:
   216→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   217→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   218→
   219→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   220→    expired = 0
   221→    for payload in assessments.values():
   222→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   223→            continue
   224→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   225→            continue
   226→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   227→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   228→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   229→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   230→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   231→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   233→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   234→        payload.pop("components", None)
   235→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   236→        expired += 1
   237→    return expired

   240→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   241→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   242→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   243→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   244→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   245→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   246→    path = Path(args.path)
   247→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   248→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   249→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   250→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   251→        state
   252→    )
   253→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   254→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   255→            state,
   256→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   260→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   261→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   262→
   263→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   264→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   265→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   266→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   267→
   268→    return ScanRuntime(
   269→        args=args,
   270→        state_path=state_file,
   271→        state=state,
   272→        path=path,
   273→        config=config,
   274→        lang=lang,
   275→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   276→        profile=profile,
   277→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   278→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   279→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   280→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   281→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   286→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   287→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   288→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   289→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   290→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   291→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   292→        return findings
   293→
   294→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   295→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   296→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   297→    )
   298→    if not stale:
   299→        return findings
   300→
   301→    augmented = list(findings)
   302→    augmented.extend(stale)
   303→    for stale_finding in stale:
   304→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   305→    return augmented
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   309→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→    *,
   312→    decay_scans: int,
   313→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   314→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   315→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   316→        findings,
   317→        state=runtime.state,
   318→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   319→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   320→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_scan_generation(
   325→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   326→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   327→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   328→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   329→        disable_parse_cache,
   330→        enable_parse_cache,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    enable_file_cache()
   334→    enable_parse_cache()
   335→    try:
   336→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   337→            runtime.path,
   338→            lang=runtime.lang,
   339→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   340→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   341→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   342→                profile=runtime.profile,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→    finally:
   346→        disable_parse_cache()
   347→        disable_file_cache()
   348→
   349→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   350→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   351→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   352→    )
   353→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   354→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   355→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   356→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   357→    )
   358→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   359→        findings,
   360→        runtime,
   361→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   362→    )
   363→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   364→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   365→
   366→
   367→def merge_scan_results(
   368→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   369→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   370→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   371→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   372→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   373→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   374→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   375→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   376→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   377→
   378→    if not path_changed:
   379→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   380→    else:
   381→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   382→    prev_dim_scores = (
   383→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   387→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   388→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   389→
   390→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   391→
   392→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   393→        runtime.state,
   394→        findings,
   395→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   396→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   397→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   398→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   399→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   400→            potentials=potentials,
   401→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   402→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   403→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   404→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   405→        ),
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   409→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   410→    )
   411→    state_mod.save_state(
   412→        runtime.state,
   413→        runtime.state_path,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    return ScanMergeResult(
   418→        diff=diff,
   419→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   420→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   421→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   422→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   423→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   424→    )
   425→
   426→
   427→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   428→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   429→    config: dict[str, object],
   430→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   431→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   432→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   433→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   434→    )
   435→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   436→    open_findings = [
   437→        finding
   438→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   439→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   440→        ).values()
   441→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   442→    ]
   443→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   444→        open_findings,
   445→        budget=noise_budget,
   446→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   447→    )
   448→
   449→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   450→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   451→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   452→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   453→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   454→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   455→    )
   456→
   457→
   458→def persist_reminder_history(
   459→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   460→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   461→) -> None:
   462→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   463→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   464→        return
   465→
   466→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   467→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   468→    state_mod.save_state(
   469→        runtime.state,
   470→        runtime.state_path,
   471→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   472→    )
   473→
   474→
   475→__all__ = [
   476→    "ScanStateContractError",
   477→    "ScanMergeResult",
   478→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   479→    "ScanRuntime",
   480→    "merge_scan_results",
   481→    "persist_reminder_history",
   482→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   483→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   484→    "run_scan_generation",
   485→]
   486→
```

> TOOL

tool_result Bash
```
usage: desloppify [-h] [--lang LANG] [--exclude PATTERN]
                  {scan,status,tree,show,next,resolve,ignore,fix,plan,viz,detect,move,review,zone,config,dev,langs,update-skill}
                  ...

Desloppify — codebase health tracker

positional arguments:
  {scan,status,tree,show,next,resolve,ignore,fix,plan,viz,detect,move,review,zone,config,dev,langs,update-skill}
    scan                Run all detectors, update state, show diff
    status              Score dashboard with per-tier progress
    tree                Annotated codebase tree (text)
    show                Dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or ID
    next                Show next highest-priority open finding
    resolve             Mark finding(s) as fixed/wontfix/false_positive/open
    ignore              Add pattern to ignore list, remove matching findings
    fix                 Auto-fix mechanical issues
    plan                Generate prioritized markdown plan from state
    viz                 Generate interactive HTML treemap
    detect              Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    move                Move a file or directory and update all import
                        references
    review              Prepare or import holistic subjective review
    zone                Show/set/clear zone classifications
    config              Show/set/unset project configuration
    dev                 Developer utilities
    langs               List all available language plugins with depth and
                        tools
    update-skill        Install or update the desloppify skill/agent document

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --lang LANG           Language to scan (bash, clojure, csharp, cxx, dart,
                        elixir, erlang, fsharp, gdscript, go, haskell, java,
                        javascript, kotlin, lua, nim, ocaml, perl, php,
                        powershell, python, r, ruby, rust, scala, swift,
                        typescript, zig). Auto-detected if omitted.
  --exclude PATTERN     Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match;
                        repeatable)

workflow:
  scan                          Run all detectors, update state, show diff
  status                        Score dashboard with per-tier progress
  tree                          Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
  show <pattern>                Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
  resolve <pattern> <status>    Mark findings as open/fixed/wontfix/false_positive
  ignore <pattern>              Suppress findings matching a pattern
  zone show                     Show zone classifications for all files
  zone set <file> <zone>        Override zone for a file
  review --prepare              Prepare holistic codebase review data
  review --import FILE          Import review findings from JSON
  review --validate-import FILE Validate import payload/trust mode without mutating state
  review --external-start       Start external blind review session (Claude cloud)
  review --external-submit      Submit external session results with canonical provenance
  review --merge                Merge duplicate review findings
  plan                          Generate prioritized markdown plan

examples:
  desloppify scan --skip-slow
  desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
  desloppify tree --focus shared/components --sort findings --depth 3
  desloppify tree --detail --focus shared/components/MediaLightbox --min-loc 300
  desloppify show src/shared/components/PromptEditorModal.tsx
  desloppify show gods
  desloppify show "src/shared/components/MediaLightbox"
  desloppify resolve fixed "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" --note "removed unused React import" --attest "I have actually removed the unused React import from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify resolve fixed "logs::src/foo.tsx::*" --note "removed debug logs" --attest "I have actually removed all console.log calls from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify resolve wontfix deprecated --note "migration in progress" --attest "I have actually verified these are tracked in the migration plan and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify ignore "smells::*::async_no_await" --attest "I have actually verified these are intentional fire-and-forget patterns and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify detect logs --top 10
  desloppify detect dupes --threshold 0.9
  desloppify review --validate-import findings.json --attested-external --attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness of overall score and is unbiased."
  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude
  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <session_id> --import findings.json
  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --retrospective
  desloppify dev scaffold-lang go --extension .go --marker go.mod --default-src .
  desloppify move src/shared/hooks/useFoo.ts src/shared/hooks/video/useFoo.ts --dry-run
  desloppify move scripts/foo/bar.py scripts/foo/baz/bar.py
```

So `lang_capabilities` is not even listed as a field in `StateModel` at all. The code in `_state_lang_capabilities` is accessing a key that is not part of the TypedDict schema, which is a broader concern. But the finding's specific claim about the scan_workflow.py helpers silently swallowing bad state is partially wrong -- two of the three actually do raise errors on bad shapes, only `lang_capabilities` does the silent fallback, and that field is truly optional (not even in the schema).

**Step 14: Let me also verify the import_helpers.py side. The finding says `_normalize_import_payload_shape` uses `.get()` + defaults. Let me re-examine it.**

I already read it. Looking at lines 58-126, I can see the pattern: it uses `.get()` on fields, checks types, records errors, but still substitutes defaults and continues. So if `findings` is not a list, it logs an error AND sets `findings = []`, then at line 113-114, if there are errors, it returns `None, errors`. So the function does validate -- it just does so in a defensive way.

The finding claims this "masks schema drift and invalid runtime shapes." That is a valid design critique: the function could use `[]` indexing (which TypedDicts support) to crash hard on missing keys rather than `.get()`. But the actual code does collect errors and return them. It is not silently swallowing bad data.

```
usage: desloppify [-h] [--lang LANG] [--exclude PATTERN]
                  {scan,status,tree,show,next,resolve,ignore,fix,plan,viz,detect,move,review,zone,config,dev,langs,update-skill}
                  ...

Desloppify — codebase health tracker

positional arguments:
  {scan,status,tree,show,next,resolve,ignore,fix,plan,viz,detect,move,review,zone,config,dev,langs,update-skill}
    scan                Run all detectors, update state, show diff
    status              Score dashboard with per-tier progress
    tree                Annotated codebase tree (text)
    show                Dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or ID
    next                Show next highest-priority open finding
    resolve             Mark finding(s) as fixed/wontfix/false_positive/open
    ignore              Add pattern to ignore list, remove matching findings
    fix                 Auto-fix mechanical issues
    plan                Generate prioritized markdown plan from state
    viz                 Generate interactive HTML treemap
    detect              Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    move                Move a file or directory and update all import
                        references
    review              Prepare or import holistic subjective review
    zone                Show/set/clear zone classifications
    config              Show/set/unset project configuration
    dev                 Developer utilities
    langs               List all available language plugins with depth and
                        tools
    update-skill        Install or update the desloppify skill/agent document

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --lang LANG           Language to scan (bash, clojure, csharp, cxx, dart,
                        elixir, erlang, fsharp, gdscript, go, haskell, java,
                        javascript, kotlin, lua, nim, ocaml, perl, php,
                        powershell, python, r, ruby, rust, scala, swift,
                        typescript, zig). Auto-detected if omitted.
  --exclude PATTERN     Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match;
                        repeatable)

workflow:
  scan                          Run all detectors, update state, show diff
  status                        Score dashboard with per-tier progress
  tree                          Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
  show <pattern>                Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
  resolve <pattern> <status>    Mark findings as open/fixed/wontfix/false_positive
  ignore <pattern>              Suppress findings matching a pattern
  zone show                     Show zone classifications for all files
  zone set <file> <zone>        Override zone for a file
  review --prepare              Prepare holistic codebase review data
  review --import FILE          Import review findings from JSON
  review --validate-import FILE Validate import payload/trust mode without mutating state
  review --external-start       Start external blind review session (Claude cloud)
  review --external-submit      Submit external session results with canonical provenance
  review --merge                Merge duplicate review findings
  plan                          Generate prioritized markdown plan

examples:
  desloppify scan --skip-slow
  desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
  desloppify tree --focus shared/components --sort findings --depth 3
  desloppify tree --detail --focus shared/components/MediaLightbox --min-loc 300
  desloppify show src/shared/components/PromptEditorModal.tsx
  desloppify show gods
  desloppify show "src/shared/components/MediaLightbox"
  desloppify resolve fixed "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" --note "removed unused React import" --attest "I have actually removed the unused React import from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify resolve fixed "logs::src/foo.tsx::*" --note "removed debug logs" --attest "I have actually removed all console.log calls from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify resolve wontfix deprecated --note "migration in progress" --attest "I have actually verified these are tracked in the migration plan and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify ignore "smells::*::async_no_await" --attest "I have actually verified these are intentional fire-and-forget patterns and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
  desloppify detect logs --top 10
  desloppify detect dupes --threshold 0.9
  desloppify review --validate-import findings.json --attested-external --attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness of overall score and is unbiased."
  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude
  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <session_id> --import findings.json
  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --retrospective
  desloppify dev scaffold-lang go --extension .go --marker go.mod --default-src .
  desloppify move src/shared/hooks/useFoo.ts src/shared/hooks/video/useFoo.ts --dry-run
  desloppify move scripts/foo/bar.py scripts/foo/baz/bar.py
```

Now I have enough data to write the meta-report. Let me compile everything.

---

## META-REPORT: First-Time Experience with Desloppify

### What I Did (step by step, what commands I ran and why)

1. **`desloppify --help`** -- To understand what commands exist. This was successful and gave me a good overview with examples.

2. **`desloppify --lang python status`** -- My first instinct to check "health status." This was the most information-dense output of the entire session. It showed scores, dimensions, a structural debt breakdown, and coaching advice.

3. **`desloppify --lang python plan`** -- Followed the memory instructions to run `plan`. Output was massive (156KB), got truncated. It gave me a full tier-by-tier breakdown of every open finding.

4. **`desloppify --lang python next`** -- To see the single top-priority item. Got a clear recommendation about TypedDict contract softening.

5. **`desloppify show smells --status open --top 10`** -- To explore the second-biggest score drag. Found monster functions, empty except blocks, swallowed errors.

6. **`desloppify show security --status open --top 10`** -- To understand the 59 security findings. Found mostly Bandit warnings (subprocess, assert, pseudo-random) -- low severity.

7. **`desloppify show structural --status open --top 10`** -- To understand file health. Found files that "need decomposition" (300-500 LOC range).

8. **`desloppify show review --status open`** -- To see the 10 holistic review findings, which turned out to be the most impactful items.

9. **`desloppify show subjective`** -- This **failed silently** -- returned "No open findings matching: subjective" which was confusing because the status output clearly shows subjective dimensions.

10. **`desloppify next --count 20`** -- To see a fuller list of prioritized work. Good output.

11. **`desloppify show orphaned --status open`** -- Following the tool's "AGENT PLAN" suggestion. Found 1 orphaned file.

12. **`desloppify tree --depth 2`** -- To see the codebase structure with finding annotations.

13. **`desloppify show test_health --top 10`** -- To understand test coverage gaps. Showed files without direct tests.

14. **`desloppify show duplication --status open`** -- To check duplication. Only 3 findings, nearly perfect.

15. **`desloppify config`** -- To see configuration. Found the target strict score is 98.

16. **`desloppify status --json`** -- To get structured data and understand the score formula.

17. **`desloppify show --chronic`** -- To find recurring problems. Found 15 findings that keep getting auto-resolved then reappearing.

18. **Various --help calls** on subcommands.

---

### What Confused Me

1. **Four different scores with unclear distinctions.** The status output shows `overall 90.6/100`, `objective 95.1/100`, `strict 90.6/100`, `verified 95.1/100`. The JSON shows `overall_score`, `objective_score`, `strict_score`, `verified_strict_score`. At no point does the tool explain what these mean or how they differ. I had to reverse-engineer from the JSON that:
   - `objective` = mechanical detectors only
   - `overall` = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective
   - `strict` = penalizes both open AND wontfix findings
   - `verified` = unclear, seems to equal objective?

   This is the single biggest comprehension gap in the tool.

2. **"Subjective" vs "Mechanical" is never defined.** I eventually figured out that mechanical dimensions (File health, Code quality, Duplication, Test health, Security) are computed from automated detectors, while subjective dimensions (Abstraction fit, Contracts, etc.) come from AI review. But this distinction is never stated.

3. **The "checks" count of 10 for all subjective dimensions.** Every subjective dimension has exactly 10 checks. Why 10? Is it arbitrary? This makes the subjective scores feel synthetic compared to mechanical dimensions with thousands of checks.

4. **Tier meanings are inconsistent with actual content.** Tier 1 is labeled "Auto-fixable" but the top 5 T1 items are all holistic review findings that require design judgment. Only 1 of 6 T1 items is actually auto-fixable (an unused import). The tier label and content are contradictory.

5. **`show subjective` returns nothing.** The status command prominently shows subjective dimensions, but `show subjective` matches no findings. The actual command to see them is `show review --status open`. There is no way I could have discovered this without the coaching hint in the status output.

6. **"Noise budget" is confusing.** Messages like `Noise budget: 10/detector (133 hidden: smells: +133)` appear throughout. It means there are 143 total smell findings but only 10 are shown. I had to guess this -- it is never explained and the word "budget" is misleading (it sounds like I have 10 remaining, not that 10 is the cap).

7. **The weight system in the score breakdown.** Elegance dimensions have weights of 22, Contracts has 12, Type safety has 12, Abstraction fit has 8, Error consistency has 3, many have 1. These weights dramatically affect the overall score but are invisible in the normal `status` output. I only found them by digging into `--json`.

8. **"In-scope" vs "out-of-scope" vs "global" open counts.** Status shows `open (in-scope): 728 / open (out-of-scope carried): 3680 / open (global): 4408`. What are "out-of-scope carried" findings? Carried from where? This is never explained.

---

### What Was Missing

1. **A glossary or `--explain` flag.** There is no way to ask "what does File health mean?" or "what detectors contribute to Code quality?" from the CLI. I had to dig into JSON output.

2. **Score trend over time.** Status mentions "Score plateaued at 90.6 for 5 scans" but there is no history command or graph. I would want to see: "Scan #262: 89.2, #263: 90.1, #264: 90.6, ..." etc.

3. **A "summary for humans" mode.** All outputs are formatted for agents/developers. There is no high-level "here is what your codebase looks like in plain English" summary. The closest is the coaching text in `status`, which is good but buried at the bottom.

4. **Impact estimation for individual findings.** When I see an open finding, I want to know "if I fix this, how much will my score go up?" The score breakdown JSON has the math, but the tool never surfaces "fixing this finding is worth +X points."

5. **Explanation of what "review" entails.** Many dimensions say "Action: review" but the tool never explains what this means in practice. The command `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel` is suggested, but what is codex? What does it cost? How long does it take? This feels like a major workflow step that is barely documented.

6. **A way to see ALL findings at once** (not filtered by detector). `show` always requires a pattern, and even with patterns, results are capped by noise budget. There is no `show --all --status open` that I could find.

---

### What Stopped Me

1. **The `plan` output was too large to read.** 156KB is not scannable. I had to use `head -200` to get anything useful. The plan should have a `--summary` mode.

2. **`show subjective` dead end.** Wasted time trying different patterns before realizing subjective scores are not "findings" in the normal sense.

3. **The tool warns "Tool code changed since last scan" on every command.** This warning appeared on literally every command I ran and it was never relevant to my exploration. It is noisy.

4. **No error for invalid patterns.** When `show` gets a pattern that matches nothing, it just silently returns 0 results. It would be helpful if it suggested similar patterns.

---

### What the Output Told Me vs What I Actually Needed

**What the output told me:**
- Overall score is 90.6/100 (strict) with a target of 98
- 733 open findings across 641 files and 126K LOC
- The biggest drags are Abstraction fit (48%), Contracts (65%), and Error consistency (65.3%)
- 143 code smells, 131 structural issues, and 218 files need subjective review
- The tool recommends running holistic reviews to improve subjective scores
- 5 Tier 1 review findings need design-level fixes

**What I actually needed to understand the health:**
- **Is this codebase in good shape?** Probably yes -- 90.6 is high, mechanical score is 95.1. The main weakness is in subjective/design dimensions.
- **What are the real problems?** A few files are very large (400-1400 LOC), there are broad exception handlers, and the architecture has some contract-level inconsistencies identified by holistic review.
- **What should I fix first?** The tool is clear about this: fix the 5 T1 review findings (design issues), then work on smells and structural decomposition.
- **Is it getting better or worse?** Plateaued for 5 scans. Not getting worse, but not improving either.

The gap: the tool tells me a lot about individual findings but makes me work hard to understand the big picture. The "Score recipe" section in `status` is the best part, but it requires understanding the weight system to interpret.

---

### My First Impression of the Tool

**Strengths:**
- The `status` command is genuinely excellent. The dimension table, structural debt by area, and coaching recommendations are well-designed.
- Finding IDs are precise and unambiguous. The resolve/wontfix workflow is well thought out.
- The `next` command is exactly what an agent needs: "here is the one thing to do next."
- Code quality and duplication detection seem thorough and accurate.
- The attestation system for resolving findings is clever and prevents gaming.
- The noise budget prevents information overload (even if it is confusing at first).

**Weaknesses:**
- Too many score variants without explanation (overall vs objective vs strict vs verified).
- The subjective scoring system feels opaque -- weights are hidden, dimensions like "Elegance" have 3 sub-categories with weight 22 each (total 56 out of 123 subjective weight), dominating the score silently.
- Tier labels do not match tier content (T1 "auto-fixable" contains design review items).
- The tool assumes you already understand its concepts. There is no onboarding path.
- Output is designed for agents, not for humans trying to understand their codebase for the first time.

---

### Suggestions for Improvement

1. **Add a `desloppify explain` command.** `desloppify explain scores` should explain what overall/objective/strict/verified mean. `desloppify explain "File health"` should explain that dimension. `desloppify explain tiers` should explain the tier system.

2. **Show score impact per finding.** Even an approximate "+0.3 pts if fixed" next to each finding would dramatically improve prioritization.

3. **Fix the Tier 1 labeling.** If holistic review findings are T1, the tier label should not say "Auto-fixable." Either relabel or re-tier.

4. **Add `plan --summary`.** A 1-page version of the 156KB plan that shows just the dimension table, tier counts, and top 10 items.

5. **Surface dimension weights in the normal `status` output.** Even just a "Weight" column in the dimension table would help. The current output hides the single most important factor in the score calculation.

6. **Explain "noise budget" on first encounter.** Something like "Showing 10 of 143 (capped at 10 per detector -- use `config set finding_noise_budget 0` for all)."

7. **Suppress the "tool code changed" warning** or show it only once per session, not on every command.

8. **Make `show subjective` actually work.** It should either show subjective dimension details or clearly redirect to the right command.

9. **Add score history.** `desloppify history` showing the last N scan scores with dates would help track progress.

10. **Add a `--human` or `--brief` output mode** that gives a plain-English summary instead of the detailed agent-oriented output.
