```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    11→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    12→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    17→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    18→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    19→
    20→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    21→        return
    22→
    23→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    24→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    25→
    26→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    27→    if output:
    28→        try:
    29→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    30→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    31→            print_agent_plan(["Inspect and execute the generated plan."], next_command=next_command)
    32→        except OSError as e:
    33→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    34→    else:
    35→        print(plan_md)
    36→        print()
    37→        print_agent_plan(["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."], next_command=next_command)
    38→
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→
```

```
    41→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    51→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)

    53→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    54→        print(

    61→                    subjective_measures,
    62→                    suppression,
    63→                ),
    64→                indent=2,
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    73→    if stale_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))

    76→    if skill_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)

    85→    narrative = compute_narrative(

   100→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   101→

   113→        show_subjective_followup(
   114→            state,
   115→            dim_scores,

   125→        print()

   133→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))

   141→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,

   148→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   149→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   150→    )
   151→
   152→
   153→def _status_json_payload(
   154→    state: dict,
   155→    stats: dict,
   156→    dim_scores: dict,
   157→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   158→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   159→    suppression: dict,
   160→) -> dict:
   161→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   162→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   163→    open_scope = (
   164→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   165→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   166→        else None
   167→    )
   168→    return {
   169→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   170→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   171→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   172→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   173→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   174→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   175→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   176→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
```

```
1→"""Headline computation for terminal display."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import _history_strict
     6→
     7→
     8→def _compute_headline(
     9→    phase: str,
    10→    dimensions: dict,
    11→    debt: dict,
    12→    milestone: str | None,
    13→    diff: dict | None,
    14→    obj_strict: float | None,
    15→    obj_score: float | None,
    16→    stats: dict,
    17→    history: list[dict],
    18→    open_by_detector: dict | None = None,
    19→) -> str | None:
    20→    """Compute one computed sentence for terminal display."""
    21→    # Security callout prefix — prepended to any headline when security findings exist
    22→    security_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("security", 0)
    23→    security_prefix = ""
    24→    if security_count > 0:
    25→        s = "s" if security_count != 1 else ""
    26→        security_prefix = f"\u26a0 {security_count} security finding{s} — review before other cleanup. "
    27→
    28→    # Review findings callout — all phases
    29→    review_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review", 0)
    30→    review_suffix = ""
    31→    if review_count > 0:
    32→        s = "s" if review_count != 1 else ""
    33→        uninvestigated = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review_uninvestigated", 0)
    34→        if uninvestigated > 0:
    35→            review_suffix = (
    36→                f" ({review_count} review finding{s} \u2014 run `desloppify show review --status open`)"
    37→            )
    38→        else:
    39→            review_suffix = f" ({review_count} review finding{s} pending)"
    40→
    41→    headline = _compute_headline_inner(
    42→        phase, dimensions, debt, milestone, diff, obj_strict, obj_score, stats, history
    43→    )
    44→
    45→    if headline is None and not security_prefix and not review_suffix:
    46→        return None
    47→    parts = security_prefix + (headline or "") + review_suffix
    48→    return parts or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _compute_headline_inner(
    52→    phase: str,
    53→    dimensions: dict,
    54→    debt: dict,
    55→    milestone: str | None,
    56→    diff: dict | None,
    57→    strict_score: float | None,
    58→    overall_score: float | None,
    59→    stats: dict,
    60→    history: list[dict],
    61→) -> str | None:
    62→    """Compute the base headline (without security prefix)."""
    63→    # Milestone takes priority
    64→    if milestone:
    65→        return milestone
    66→
    67→    # First scan framing
    68→    if phase == "first_scan":
    69→        dims = len(dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])) if dimensions else 0
    70→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    71→        if dims:
    72→            return f"First scan complete. {open_count} open findings across {dims} dimensions."
    73→        return f"First scan complete. {open_count} findings detected."
    74→
    75→    # Regression — acknowledge that drops after fixes are normal
    76→    if phase == "regression":
    77→        prev = _history_strict(history[-2])
    78→        curr = _history_strict(history[-1])
    79→        if prev is not None and curr is not None:
    80→            drop = round(prev - curr, 1)
    81→            return (
    82→                f"Score shifted {drop} pts — this is normal after structural changes. "
    83→                f"Rescan after your next fix to see the real trend."
    84→            )
    85→
    86→    # Stagnation — suggest which dimension to focus on
    87→    if phase == "stagnation":
    88→        if strict_score is not None:
    89→            stuck_scans = min(len(history), 5)
    90→            wontfix = debt.get("wontfix_count", 0)
    91→            # Point to the specific dimension dragging things down
    92→            lowest_dims = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
    93→            if lowest_dims:
    94→                dim = lowest_dims[0]
    95→                if wontfix > 0:
    96→                    return (
    97→                        f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
    98→                        f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is. "
    99→                        f"{wontfix} wontfix items may also be worth revisiting."
   100→                    )
   101→                return (
   102→                    f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   103→                    f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is."
   104→                )
   105→            if wontfix > 0:
   106→                return (
   107→                    f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   108→                    f"{wontfix} wontfix items — revisit?"
   109→                )
   110→            return (
   111→                f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   112→                f"Try tackling a different dimension."
   113→            )
   114→
   115→    # Leverage point (lowest dimension with biggest impact)
   116→    lowest = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
   117→    if lowest and lowest[0].get("impact", 0) > 0:
   118→        top = lowest[0]
   119→        return (
   120→            f"{top['name']} is your biggest lever: "
   121→            f"{top['issues']} items → +{top['impact']} pts"
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    # Gap callout
   125→    if debt.get("overall_gap", 0) > 5.0:
   126→        gap = debt["overall_gap"]
   127→        worst = debt.get("worst_dimension", "")
   128→        if strict_score is not None and overall_score is not None:
   129→            return (
   130→                f"Strict {strict_score:.1f} vs overall {overall_score:.1f} — "
   131→                f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt, mostly in {worst}"
   132→            )
   133→
   134→    # Maintenance phase
   135→    if phase == "maintenance":
   136→        return (
   137→            f"Strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 — maintenance mode. Watch for regressions."
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    # Middle grind fallback — point toward next item
   141→    if phase == "middle_grind":
   142→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
   143→        if lowest:
   144→            top = lowest[0]
   145→            return (
   146→                f"{open_count} findings open. {top['name']} ({top['strict']}%) "
   147→                f"needs attention — run `desloppify next` to start."
   148→            )
   149→        if open_count > 0:
   150→            return f"{open_count} findings open. Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item."
   151→
   152→    # Early momentum fallback — celebrate trajectory
   153→    if phase == "early_momentum" and strict_score is not None:
   154→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
   155→        return f"Score {strict_score:.1f}/100 with {open_count} findings open. Keep the momentum going."
   156→
   157→    return None
   158→
```

```
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (
    24→        overall_score is not None
    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )

    92→        if isinstance(findings, dict)

   107→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   108→            "by_tier": by_tier,

   110→            "suppression": suppression,

   112→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   113→            "open_scope": open_scope,

   115→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),

   121→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:

   123→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   124→    for p in ignores[:10]:

   126→

   139→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):

   143→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   144→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   145→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→        print(

   150→                "dim",
   151→            )
   152→        )

   162→
   163→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   164→    dim_scores: dict,
   165→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   166→) -> str | None:
   167→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   168→    lowest_name = None
   169→    lowest_score = 101.0
   170→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   171→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   172→        if not ds:
   173→            continue
   174→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   175→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   176→            lowest_score = strict_val
   177→            lowest_name = dim.name
   178→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   179→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   180→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   181→            lowest_score = strict_val
   182→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   183→    return lowest_name
   184→
   185→
   186→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   187→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   188→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   190→        return {}
   191→
   192→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   193→    for finding in findings.values():
   194→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   195→            continue
   196→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   197→            continue
   198→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   199→        dimension = ""
   200→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   201→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   202→        if not dimension:
   203→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   204→        if not dimension:
   205→            continue
   206→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   207→    return counts
   208→
   209→
   210→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   211→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   212→) -> None:
   213→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   214→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   215→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   216→        if not ds:
   217→            continue
   218→        score_val = ds["score"]
   219→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   220→        checks = ds["checks"]
   221→
   222→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   223→
   224→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   225→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   226→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   227→        print(
   228→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   229→        )
   230→
   231→
   232→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   233→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   234→    *,
   235→    lowest_name: str | None,
   236→    bar_len: int,
   237→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   238→) -> None:
   239→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   240→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   241→        return
   242→    print(
   243→        colorize(
   244→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   245→            "dim",
   246→        )
   247→    )
   248→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   249→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   250→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   251→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   252→        tier = 4
   253→
   254→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   255→
   256→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   257→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   258→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   259→        placeholder_tag = (
   260→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   261→        )
   262→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   263→        cli_keys = [
   264→            str(key).strip()
   265→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   266→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   267→        ]
   268→        if dim_key:
   269→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   270→        elif cli_keys:
   271→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   272→        else:
   273→            issue_count = 0
   274→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   275→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   276→        print(
   277→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   278→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   279→        )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict]) -> None:
   283→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   284→    print(
   285→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   286→    )
   287→    print(
   288→        colorize(
   289→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   290→            "dim",
   291→        )
   292→    )
   293→    stale_keys = [
   294→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   295→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   296→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   297→    ]
   298→    if stale_keys:
   299→        n = len(stale_keys)
   300→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   301→        print(
   302→            colorize(
   303→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   304→                f" — run `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
   305→                "yellow",
   306→            )
   307→        )
   308→
   309→
   310→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   311→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   312→    print()
   313→    bar_len = 20
   314→    print(
   315→        colorize(
   316→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   317→            "dim",
   318→        )
   319→    )
   320→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   321→
   322→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   323→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   324→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   325→
   326→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   327→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   328→        scorecard_subjective,
   329→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   330→        bar_len=bar_len,
   331→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   332→    )
   333→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective)
   334→    print()
   335→
   336→
   337→def show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   338→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   339→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   340→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   341→    if not lowest_name:
   342→        return
   343→
   344→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   345→    lowest_kind = None
   346→    lowest_score = 101.0
   347→    lowest_issues = 0
   348→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   349→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   350→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   351→            if ds:
   352→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   353→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   354→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   355→            break
   356→    else:
   357→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   358→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   359→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   360→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   361→                lowest_issues = 0
   362→                break
   363→
   364→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   365→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   366→            print(
   367→                colorize(
   368→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   369→                    "cyan",
   370→                )
   371→            )
   372→            print()
   373→            return
   374→
   375→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   376→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   377→        if target_dim:
   378→            impact = 0.0
   379→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   380→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   381→                    {
   382→                        k: {
   383→                            "score": v["score"],
   384→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   385→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   386→                        }
   387→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   388→                        if "score" in v
   389→                    },
   390→                    potentials,
   391→                    det,
   392→                    lowest_issues,
   393→                )
   394→                if impact > 0:
   395→                    break
   396→
   397→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   398→            print(
   399→                colorize(
   400→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   401→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→

   408→def show_subjective_followup(
   409→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, target_strict_score: float
   410→) -> None:
   411→    """Show concrete subjective follow-up commands when applicable."""
   412→    if not dim_scores:
   413→        return
   414→
   415→    subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   416→    if not subjective:

   419→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   420→        state,

   423→        max_quality_items=3,
   424→        max_integrity_items=5,

   426→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):

   448→
   449→def _collect_structural_areas(
   450→    state: dict,
   451→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   452→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   453→
   454→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   455→    """
   456→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   457→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   458→    )
   459→
   460→    structural = [
   461→        f
   462→        for f in findings.values()
   463→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   464→    ]
   465→
   466→    if len(structural) < 5:
   467→        return None
   468→
   469→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   470→    for f in structural:
   471→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   472→        areas[area].append(f)
   473→
   474→    if len(areas) < 2:
   475→        return None
   476→
   477→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   478→
   479→
   480→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   481→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   482→    rows = []
   483→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   484→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   485→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   486→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   487→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   488→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   489→        rows.append(
   490→            [
   491→                area,
   492→                str(len(area_findings)),
   493→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   494→                str(open_count),
   495→                str(debt_count),
   496→                str(weight),
   497→            ]
   498→        )
   499→    return rows
   500→
   501→
   502→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   503→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   504→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   505→    if remaining > 0:
   506→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   507→
   508→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   509→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   510→    print(
   511→        colorize(
   512→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   513→        )
   514→    )
   515→    print(
   516→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   517→    )
   518→    print()
   519→
   520→
   521→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   522→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   523→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   524→    if sorted_areas is None:
   525→        return
   526→
   527→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   528→    print(
   529→        colorize(
   530→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   531→            "dim",
   532→        )
   533→    )
   534→    print()
   535→
   536→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   537→    print_table(
   538→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   539→    )
   540→
   541→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   542→
   543→
   544→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   545→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   546→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   547→    review_open = [
   548→        f
   549→        for f in findings.values()
   550→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   551→    ]
   552→    if not review_open:
   553→        return
   554→    uninvestigated = sum(
   555→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   556→    )
   557→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   558→    if uninvestigated:
   559→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   560→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   561→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   562→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   563→        print(
   564→            colorize(
   565→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   566→                "dim",
   567→            )
   568→        )
   569→    print()
```

```
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))

    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))

    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
```

```
1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def _coerce_coverage_confidence(value: object, *, default: float = 1.0) -> float:
    11→    try:
    12→        parsed = float(value)
    13→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    14→        return default
    15→    return max(0.0, min(1.0, parsed))
    16→
    17→
    18→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
    19→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
    20→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
    21→        return {}
    22→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
    23→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
    24→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
    25→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
    26→    return {}
    27→
    28→
    29→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
    30→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
    31→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
    32→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
    33→        return []
    34→
    35→    warnings: list[str] = []
    36→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
    40→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
    41→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
    42→            continue
    43→
    44→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
    45→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
    46→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    47→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
    48→
    49→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
    50→        if impact:
    51→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
    52→        if remediation:
    53→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
    54→        warnings.append(line)
    55→    return warnings
    56→
    57→
    58→def show_post_scan_analysis(
    59→    diff: dict,
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    lang,
    62→    *,
    63→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
    64→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    65→    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    66→    # Critical warnings only (reopened, cascading, chronic, coverage reduction)
    67→    warnings: list[str] = []
    68→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
    69→        warnings.append(
    70→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?"
    71→        )
    72→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
    73→        warnings.append(
    74→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
    75→        )
    76→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
    77→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
    78→    if chronic_count > 0:
    79→        warnings.append(
    80→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — "
    81→            "run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
    82→        )
```

```
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open":
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   135→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   136→        )
   137→    return by_detector
   138→
   139→
   140→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   141→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   142→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   143→    config = {}
   144→    try:
   145→        config = _load_config()
   146→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   147→        config = {}
   148→
   149→    raw_path = default_rel
   150→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   151→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   152→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   153→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   154→
   155→    path = Path(raw_path)
   156→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   157→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   158→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   159→
   160→    try:
   161→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   162→    except ValueError:
   163→        rel_path = str(path)
   164→    return rel_path, path
   165→
   166→
   167→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   168→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   169→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   170→
   171→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   172→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   173→
   174→    in_readme = False
   175→    if generated:
   176→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   177→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   178→            if readme_path.exists():
   179→                try:
   180→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   181→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   182→                    )
   183→                except OSError:
   184→                    in_readme = False
   185→                break
   186→
   187→    recommendation = None
   188→    if generated and not in_readme:
   189→        recommendation = (
   190→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   191→        )
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "generated": generated,
   195→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   196→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   197→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   198→    }
   199→
   200→
   201→def _compute_primary_action(actions: list[dict]) -> PrimaryAction | None:
   202→    """Pick the highest-priority action for user-facing guidance."""
   203→    if not actions:
   204→        return None
   205→    top = actions[0]
   206→    command = str(top.get("command", "")).strip()
   207→    if not command:
   208→        return None
   209→    description = str(top.get("description", "")).strip() or "run highest-impact action"
   210→    return {
   211→        "command": command,
   212→        "description": description,
   213→    }
   214→
   215→
   216→def _compute_why_now(
   217→    phase: str,
   218→    strategy: dict[str, object],
   219→    primary_action: dict | None,
   220→) -> str:
   221→    """Explain why the recommended action should happen now."""
   222→    hint = str(strategy.get("hint", "")).strip() if isinstance(strategy, dict) else ""
   223→    if hint:
   224→        return hint
   225→    if primary_action and primary_action.get("description"):
   226→        return str(primary_action["description"])
   227→    phase_default = {
   228→        "first_scan": "Start with highest-impact findings to establish a clean baseline.",
   229→        "regression": "Recent regressions should be contained before new work.",
   230→        "stagnation": "Current approach is stalling; tackle a different high-impact lane.",
   231→        "maintenance": "Keep the codebase stable by resolving new risk quickly.",
   232→    }
   233→    return phase_default.get(phase, "Address the highest-impact open findings first.")
   234→
   235→
   236→def _compute_verification_step(_command: str | None) -> VerificationStep:
   237→    """Verification step returned with every narrative plan."""
   238→    return {
   239→        "command": "desloppify scan",
   240→        "reason": "revalidate after changes",
   241→    }
   242→
   243→
   244→def _compute_risk_flags(state: StateModel, debt: dict) -> list[RiskFlag]:
   245→    """Build ordered risk flags from suppression and wontfix debt signals."""
   246→    flags: list[RiskFlag] = []
   247→
   248→    ignore_integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {})
   249→    suppressed_pct = float(ignore_integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   250→    ignored_count = int(ignore_integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   251→    if (
   252→        suppressed_pct >= _HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD
   253→        or ignored_count >= 100
   254→    ):
   255→        severity = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 40.0 or ignored_count >= 200 else "medium"
   256→        message = (
   257→            f"{suppressed_pct:.1f}% findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   258→            if suppressed_pct > 0
   259→            else f"{ignored_count} findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   260→        )
   261→        flags.append(
   262→            {
   263→                "type": "high_ignore_suppression",
   264→                "severity": severity,
   265→                "message": message,
   266→            }
   267→        )
   268→
   269→    wontfix_count = int(debt.get("wontfix_count", 0) or 0)
   270→    overall_gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   271→    if overall_gap >= _WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD or wontfix_count > 0:
   272→        severity = "high" if overall_gap >= 5.0 or wontfix_count >= 50 else "medium"
   273→        flags.append(
   274→            {
   275→                "type": "wontfix_gap",
   276→                "severity": severity,
   277→                "message": (
   278→                    f"Strict/lenient gap is {overall_gap:.1f} pts with "
   279→                    f"{wontfix_count} wontfix findings"
   280→                ),
   281→            }
   282→        )
   283→
   284→    flags.sort(key=lambda flag: _RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER.get(flag.get("severity"), 99))
   285→    return flags
   286→
   287→
   288→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   289→class NarrativeContext:
   290→    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
   291→
   292→    diff: dict | None = None
   293→    lang: str | None = None
   294→    command: str | None = None
   295→    config: dict | None = None
   296→
   297→def _history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   298→    if not lang:
   299→        return raw_history
   300→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   301→
   302→
   303→def _scoped_findings(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   304→    return path_scoped_findings(
   305→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   306→    )
   307→
   308→
   309→def _score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   310→    return get_strict_score(state), get_overall_score(state)
   311→
   312→
   313→def compute_narrative(
   314→    state: StateModel,
   315→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   316→) -> NarrativeResult:
   317→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
   318→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()
   319→
   320→    diff = resolved_context.diff
   321→    lang = resolved_context.lang
   322→    command = resolved_context.command
   323→    config = resolved_context.config
   324→
   325→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   326→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
   327→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   328→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   329→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
   330→    findings = _scoped_findings(state)
   331→
   332→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(findings)
   333→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
   334→
   335→    phase = _detect_phase(history, strict_score)
   336→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
   337→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, findings, history)
   338→    milestone = _detect_milestone(state, None, history)
   339→    action_context = ActionContext(
   340→        by_detector=by_detector,
   341→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
   342→        state=state,
   343→        debt=debt,
   344→        lang=lang,
   345→    )
   346→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   347→    strategy = compute_strategy(findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   348→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   349→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   350→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   351→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   352→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   353→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   354→    headline = _compute_headline(
   355→        phase,
   356→        dimensions,
   357→        debt,
   358→        milestone,
   359→        diff,
   360→        strict_score,
   361→        overall_score,
   362→        stats,
   363→        history,
   364→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   365→    )
   366→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = _compute_reminders(
   367→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   368→    )
   369→
   370→    return {
   371→        "phase": phase,
   372→        "headline": headline,
   373→        "dimensions": dimensions,
   374→        "actions": actions,
   375→        "strategy": strategy,
   376→        "tools": tools,
   377→        "debt": debt,

   379→        "primary_action": primary_action,

   383→        "strict_target": strict_target,
```

```
    14→
    15→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:
    16→    """Compute per-dimension structured analysis."""
    17→    if not dim_scores:
    18→        return {}
    19→
    20→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    21→    return {
    22→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    23→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],
    24→        "stagnant_dimensions": _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores, history),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    30→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    31→        (
    32→            (name, ds)
    33→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    34→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    35→        ),
    36→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
    37→    )
    38→
    39→    lowest = []
    40→    for name, ds in sorted_dims[:3]:
    41→        strict = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    42→        issues = ds["issues"]
    43→        impact = _dominant_detector_impact(
    44→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    45→            detectors=ds.get("detectors", {}),
    46→            potentials=potentials,
    47→        )
    48→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in ds.get("detectors", {})
    49→        entry = {
    50→            "name": name,
    51→            "strict": round(strict, 1),
    52→            "issues": issues,
    53→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    54→        }
    55→        if is_subjective:
    56→            entry["subjective"] = True
    57→            entry["impact_description"] = "re-review to improve"
    58→        lowest.append(entry)
    59→    return lowest
    60→
    61→
    62→def _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Build summary entries for dimensions with the biggest strict gap."""
    64→    biggest_gap = []
    65→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(
    66→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    67→    )
    68→    for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
    69→        lenient = ds["score"]
    70→        strict = ds.get("strict", lenient)
    71→        gap = lenient - strict
    72→        if gap > 1.0:
    73→            wontfix_count = sum(
    74→                1
    75→                for f in scoped.values()
    76→                if f["status"] == "wontfix"
    77→                and _finding_in_dimension(f, name, dim_scores)
    78→            )
    79→            biggest_gap.append(
    80→                {
    81→                    "name": name,
    82→                    "lenient": round(lenient, 1),
    83→                    "strict": round(strict, 1),
    84→                    "gap": round(gap, 1),
    85→                    "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
    86→                }
    87→            )
    88→    biggest_gap.sort(key=lambda x: -x["gap"])
    89→    return biggest_gap
    90→
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
```
