1→"""Dimension analysis and debt computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import STRUCTURAL_MERGE
     6→from desloppify.scoring import (
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    TIER_WEIGHTS,
     9→    compute_score_impact,
    10→    merge_potentials,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.state import StateModel, path_scoped_findings
    13→
    14→
    15→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:
    16→    """Compute per-dimension structured analysis."""
    17→    if not dim_scores:
    18→        return {}
    19→
    20→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    21→    return {
    22→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    23→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],
    24→        "stagnant_dimensions": _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores, history),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    30→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    31→        (
    32→            (name, ds)
    33→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    34→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    35→        ),
    36→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
    37→    )
    38→
    39→    lowest = []
    40→    for name, ds in sorted_dims[:3]:
    41→        strict = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    42→        issues = ds["issues"]
    43→        impact = _dominant_detector_impact(
    44→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    45→            detectors=ds.get("detectors", {}),
    46→            potentials=potentials,
    47→        )
    48→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in ds.get("detectors", {})
    49→        entry = {
    50→            "name": name,
    51→            "strict": round(strict, 1),
    52→            "issues": issues,
    53→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    54→        }
    55→        if is_subjective:
    56→            entry["subjective"] = True
    57→            entry["impact_description"] = "re-review to improve"
    58→        lowest.append(entry)
    59→    return lowest
    60→
    61→
    62→def _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Build summary entries for dimensions with the biggest strict gap."""
    64→    biggest_gap = []
    65→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(
    66→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    67→    )
    68→    for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
    69→        lenient = ds["score"]
    70→        strict = ds.get("strict", lenient)
    71→        gap = lenient - strict
    72→        if gap > 1.0:
    73→            wontfix_count = sum(
    74→                1
    75→                for f in scoped.values()
    76→                if f["status"] == "wontfix"
    77→                and _finding_in_dimension(f, name, dim_scores)
    78→            )
    79→            biggest_gap.append(
    80→                {
    81→                    "name": name,
    82→                    "lenient": round(lenient, 1),
    83→                    "strict": round(strict, 1),
    84→                    "gap": round(gap, 1),
    85→                    "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
    86→                }
    87→            )
    88→    biggest_gap.sort(key=lambda x: -x["gap"])
    89→    return biggest_gap
    90→
    91→
    92→def _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    93→    """Build summary entries for dimensions that have not moved recently."""
    94→    if len(history) < 3:
    95→        return []
    96→
    97→    stagnant = []
    98→    for name in dim_scores:
    99→        scores = _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name, history)
   100→        if len(scores) < 3 or max(scores) - min(scores) > 0.5:
   101→            continue
   102→        stagnant.append(
   103→            {
   104→                "name": name,
   105→                "strict": round(dim_scores[name].get("strict", dim_scores[name]["score"]), 1),
   106→                "stuck_scans": len(scores),
   107→            }
   108→        )
   109→    return stagnant
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name: str, history: list[dict]) -> list[float]:
   113→    """Collect recent strict score samples for one dimension."""
   114→    scores: list[float] = []
   115→    for history_entry in history[-5:]:
   116→        dim_entry = (history_entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(name)
   117→        if not dim_entry:
   118→            continue
   119→        strict_value = dim_entry.get("strict", dim_entry.get("score"))
   120→        if strict_value is not None:
   121→            scores.append(float(strict_value))
   122→    return scores
   123→
   124→
   125→def _dominant_detector_impact(
   126→    *,
   127→    dim_scores: dict,
   128→    detectors: dict,
   129→    potentials: dict,
   130→) -> float:
   131→    """Estimate impact using the most consequential detector in a dimension."""
   132→    normalized_scores = {
   133→        key: {
   134→            "score": value["score"],
   135→            "tier": value.get("tier", 3),
   136→            "detectors": value.get("detectors", {}),
   137→        }
   138→        for key, value in dim_scores.items()
   139→    }
   140→    impact = 0.0
   141→    for detector_name, detector_data in detectors.items():
   142→        issue_count = int(detector_data.get("issues", 0) or 0)
   143→        if issue_count <= 0:
   144→            continue
   145→        detector_impact = compute_score_impact(
   146→            normalized_scores,
   147→            potentials,
   148→            detector_name,
   149→            issue_count,
   150→        )
   151→        impact = max(impact, detector_impact)
   152→    return impact
   153→
   154→
   155→def _finding_in_dimension(finding: dict, dim_name: str, dim_scores: dict) -> bool:
   156→    """Check if a finding's detector belongs to a dimension."""
   157→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   158→    if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   159→        detector = "structural"
   160→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   161→        if dim.name == dim_name and detector in dim.detectors:
   162→            return True
   163→    return False
   164→
   165→
   166→def _analyze_debt(dim_scores: dict, findings: dict, history: list[dict]) -> dict:
   167→    """Compute wontfix debt analysis."""
   168→    # Count wontfix
   169→    wontfix_count = sum(1 for f in findings.values() if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   170→
   171→    # Compute gap per dimension
   172→    worst_dim = None
   173→    worst_gap = 0.0
   174→    overall_lenient = 0.0
   175→    overall_strict = 0.0
   176→    if dim_scores:
   177→        w_sum_l = 0.0
   178→        w_sum_s = 0.0
   179→        w_total = 0.0
   180→        for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
   181→            tier = ds.get("tier", 3)
   182→            w = TIER_WEIGHTS.get(tier, 2)
   183→            w_sum_l += ds["score"] * w
   184→            w_sum_s += ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) * w
   185→            w_total += w
   186→            gap = ds["score"] - ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   187→            if gap > worst_gap:
   188→                worst_gap = gap
   189→                worst_dim = name
   190→        if w_total > 0:
   191→            overall_lenient = round(w_sum_l / w_total, 1)
   192→            overall_strict = round(w_sum_s / w_total, 1)
   193→
   194→    overall_gap = round(overall_lenient - overall_strict, 1)
   195→
   196→    # Trend from history
   197→    trend = "stable"
   198→    if len(history) >= 3:
   199→        gaps = []
   200→        for h in history[-5:]:
   201→            hs = h.get("strict_score")
   202→            hl = h.get("overall_score")
   203→            if hs is not None and hl is not None:
   204→                gaps.append(hl - hs)
   205→        if len(gaps) >= 2:
   206→            if gaps[-1] > gaps[0] + 0.5:
   207→                trend = "growing"
   208→            elif gaps[-1] < gaps[0] - 0.5:
   209→                trend = "shrinking"
   210→
   211→    return {
   212→        "overall_gap": overall_gap,
   213→        "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
   214→        "worst_dimension": worst_dim,
   215→        "worst_gap": round(worst_gap, 1),
   216→        "trend": trend,
   217→    }
   218→

    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:

    74→            (

    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None

    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(

   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results

1→"""Scorecard dimension row helpers for the engine/planning layer.
     2→
     3→Provides ``scorecard_dimension_rows`` without importing app-layer modules.
     4→The app scorecard surface re-exports this so external call-sites are unaffected.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS
    10→
    11→
    12→def scorecard_dimension_rows(
    13→    state: dict,
    14→    *,
    15→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    17→    """Return scorecard rows using canonical dimension ordering.
    18→
    19→    Tries ``prepare_scorecard_dimensions`` from the app layer when available
    20→    (deferred import to avoid hard engine -> app dependency), then falls back
    21→    to a simple mechanical-dimension listing.
    22→    """
    23→    if dim_scores is None:
    24→        dim_scores = (
    25→            state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
    26→        )
    27→        projected_state = state
    28→    else:
    29→        projected_state = dict(state)
    30→        projected_state["dimension_scores"] = dim_scores
    31→
    32→    # Try the full app-layer scorecard projection when it can be imported.
    33→    try:
    34→        from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    35→            prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        rows = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(projected_state)
    39→        if rows:
    40→            return rows
    41→    except ImportError:
    42→        pass
    43→
    44→    # Fallback for synthetic/unit-test states without full scorecard context.
    45→    fallback_dim_scores = dim_scores or {}
    46→    if not isinstance(fallback_dim_scores, dict):
    47→        return []
    48→
    49→    mechanical_names = [dimension.name for dimension in DIMENSIONS]
    50→    fallback_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
    51→    for name in mechanical_names:
    52→        data = fallback_dim_scores.get(name)
    53→        if isinstance(data, dict):
    54→            fallback_rows.append((name, data))
    55→    fallback_rows.extend(
    56→        sorted(
    57→            [
    58→                (name, data)
    59→                for name, data in fallback_dim_scores.items()
    60→                if name not in mechanical_names and isinstance(data, dict)
    61→            ],
    62→            key=lambda item: item[0].lower(),
    63→        )
    64→    )
    65→    return fallback_rows
    66→

"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging

from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import _format_delta
from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    format_strict_target_progress,
)
from desloppify.utils import colorize

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    if not isinstance(history, list):
        return 0

    streak = 0
    for entry in reversed(history):
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            break
        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
            break
        if integrity.get("status") != status:
            break
        streak += 1
    return streak


def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    diff_parts = []
    if diff["new"]:
        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    if diff["reopened"]:
        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    if diff_parts:
        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    else:
        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
        print(
            colorize(
                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
                "yellow",
            )
        )


def show_score_delta(
    state: dict,
    prev_overall: float | None,
    prev_objective: float | None,
    prev_strict: float | None,
    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
):
    """Print the canonical score trio with deltas."""
    stats = state["stats"]
    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    scoped_open = int(stats.get("open", 0) or 0)
    out_of_scope_open = 0
    global_open = scoped_open
    if isinstance(findings, dict) and findings:
        scope_counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
        scoped_open = int(scope_counts.get("in_scope", scoped_open) or 0)
        out_of_scope_open = int(scope_counts.get("out_of_scope", 0) or 0)
        global_open = int(scope_counts.get("global", scoped_open) or 0)

    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
    wontfix_str = f" · {wontfix} wontfix" if wontfix else ""

    if (
        new.overall is None
        or new.objective is None
        or new.strict is None
        or new.verified is None
    ):
        print(
            colorize(
                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
                "yellow",
            )
        )
        return

    overall_delta_str, overall_color = _format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    objective_delta_str, objective_color = _format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    strict_delta_str, strict_color = _format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
    verified_delta_str, verified_color = _format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
    print(
        "  Scores: "
        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
        + colorize(
            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
            objective_color,
        )
        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
        + colorize(
            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
            verified_color,
        )
        + colorize(
            "  |  "
            f"open (in-scope): {scoped_open} · "
            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {out_of_scope_open} · "
            f"open (global): {global_open}"
            f"{wontfix_str} / {stats['total']} in-scope total",
            "dim",
        )
    )
    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
        print(
            colorize(
                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
                "yellow",
            )
        )

    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
        status = integrity.get("status")
        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
        target = integrity.get("target_score")
        if status == "penalized":

                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
                    "red",
                )
            )
            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
            if streak >= 2:

   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],

   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
   201→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
   202→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
   203→        effective = configured * sample_factor
   204→        mech_sum += score * effective
   205→        mech_weight += effective
   206→        mechanical_rows.append(
   207→            {
   208→                "name": str(name),
   209→                "score": score,

   212→                "configured_weight": configured,
   213→                "effective_weight": effective,
   214→            }
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→

   222→        subjective_fraction = 0.0

   231→        overall_score = round(

   291→
   292→def compute_health_score(
   293→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   294→) -> float:
   295→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   296→    return float(
   297→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   298→            "overall_score"
   299→        ]

    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    33→            if key in seen:
    34→                continue
    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
    38→
    39→
    40→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    41→    return ", ".join(
    42→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    43→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    48→    count = len(entries)
    49→    names = ", ".join(
    50→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    51→    )
    52→    if count > max_names:
    53→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    54→    return names
    55→
    56→
    57→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    58→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    59→        return 0
    60→    if isinstance(value, int):
    61→        return value
    62→    if isinstance(value, float):
    63→        return int(value)
    64→    if isinstance(value, str):
    65→        try:
    66→            return int(value.strip())
    67→        except ValueError:
    68→            return 0
    69→    return 0
    70→
    71→
    72→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    73→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    74→        return []
    75→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    76→
    77→
    78→def subjective_rerun_command(
    79→    items: list[dict],
    80→    *,

    88→    # If no evidence of prior runner usage, suggest --prepare first

    91→        if dim_keys:
    92→            command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
    93→        cmd = f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
    94→        return f"{cmd} (set up `--runner codex` for automated reviews)"
    95→

    99→        "--run-batches",
   100→        "--runner",

   102→        "--parallel",
   103→        "--scan-after-import",
   104→    ]
   105→    if dim_keys:
   106→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   107→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"

   135→            {

   137→                "score": 0.0,
   138→                "strict": 0.0,
   139→                "issues": 0,
   140→                "placeholder": False,
   141→                "cli_keys": [key],
   142→            }

     9→def score_summary_lines(

    44→    if total_files:

    46→        print(
    47→            colorize(

    50→                "dim",

    52→        )

def compute_score_impact(
    dimension_scores: dict,
    potentials: dict[str, int],
    detector: str,
    issues_to_fix: int,
) -> float:
    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    target_dim = None
    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
        if detector in dim.detectors:
            target_dim = dim
            break
    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
        return 0.0

    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    if potential <= 0:
        return 0.0

    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)

    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    if not det_data:
        return 0.0

    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)

    total_potential = 0
    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0

1→"""Headline computation for terminal display."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import _history_strict
     6→
     7→
     8→def _compute_headline(
     9→    phase: str,
    10→    dimensions: dict,
    11→    debt: dict,
    12→    milestone: str | None,
    13→    diff: dict | None,
    14→    obj_strict: float | None,
    15→    obj_score: float | None,
    16→    stats: dict,
    17→    history: list[dict],
    18→    open_by_detector: dict | None = None,
    19→) -> str | None:
    20→    """Compute one computed sentence for terminal display."""
    21→    # Security callout prefix — prepended to any headline when security findings exist
    22→    security_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("security", 0)
    23→    security_prefix = ""
    24→    if security_count > 0:
    25→        s = "s" if security_count != 1 else ""
    26→        security_prefix = f"\u26a0 {security_count} security finding{s} — review before other cleanup. "
    27→
    28→    # Review findings callout — all phases
    29→    review_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review", 0)
    30→    review_suffix = ""
    31→    if review_count > 0:
    32→        s = "s" if review_count != 1 else ""
    33→        uninvestigated = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review_uninvestigated", 0)
    34→        if uninvestigated > 0:
    35→            review_suffix = (
    36→                f" ({review_count} review finding{s} \u2014 run `desloppify show review --status open`)"
    37→            )
    38→        else:
    39→            review_suffix = f" ({review_count} review finding{s} pending)"
    40→
    41→    headline = _compute_headline_inner(
    42→        phase, dimensions, debt, milestone, diff, obj_strict, obj_score, stats, history
    43→    )
    44→
    45→    if headline is None and not security_prefix and not review_suffix:
    46→        return None
    47→    parts = security_prefix + (headline or "") + review_suffix
    48→    return parts or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _compute_headline_inner(
    52→    phase: str,
    53→    dimensions: dict,
    54→    debt: dict,
    55→    milestone: str | None,
    56→    diff: dict | None,
    57→    strict_score: float | None,
    58→    overall_score: float | None,
    59→    stats: dict,
    60→    history: list[dict],
    61→) -> str | None:
    62→    """Compute the base headline (without security prefix)."""
    63→    # Milestone takes priority
    64→    if milestone:
    65→        return milestone
    66→
    67→    # First scan framing
    68→    if phase == "first_scan":
    69→        dims = len(dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])) if dimensions else 0
    70→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    71→        if dims:
    72→            return f"First scan complete. {open_count} open findings across {dims} dimensions."
    73→        return f"First scan complete. {open_count} findings detected."
    74→
    75→    # Regression — acknowledge that drops after fixes are normal
    76→    if phase == "regression":
    77→        prev = _history_strict(history[-2])
    78→        curr = _history_strict(history[-1])
    79→        if prev is not None and curr is not None:
    80→            drop = round(prev - curr, 1)
    81→            return (
    82→                f"Score shifted {drop} pts — this is normal after structural changes. "
    83→                f"Rescan after your next fix to see the real trend."
    84→            )
    85→
    86→    # Stagnation — suggest which dimension to focus on
    87→    if phase == "stagnation":
    88→        if strict_score is not None:
    89→            stuck_scans = min(len(history), 5)
    90→            wontfix = debt.get("wontfix_count", 0)
    91→            # Point to the specific dimension dragging things down
    92→            lowest_dims = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
    93→            if lowest_dims:
    94→                dim = lowest_dims[0]
    95→                if wontfix > 0:
    96→                    return (
    97→                        f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
    98→                        f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is. "
    99→                        f"{wontfix} wontfix items may also be worth revisiting."
   100→                    )

    54→
    55→
    56→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    57→    """Print the scan header line."""
    58→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    63→    lines = [
    64→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    66→    ]
    67→    print("\n".join(lines))
    68→
    69→
    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    71→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    72→    if not effective_include_slow:
    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.hidden_total:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    80→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    81→                "dim",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    try:
   111→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   112→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   113→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   116→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   117→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   124→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   125→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   128→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   129→        print(
   130→            colorize(
   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→

1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def _coerce_coverage_confidence(value: object, *, default: float = 1.0) -> float:
    11→    try:
    12→        parsed = float(value)
    13→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    14→        return default
    15→    return max(0.0, min(1.0, parsed))
    16→
    17→
    18→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
    19→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
    20→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
    21→        return {}
    22→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
    23→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
    24→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
    25→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
    26→    return {}
    27→
    28→
    29→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
    30→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
    31→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
    32→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
    33→        return []
    34→
    35→    warnings: list[str] = []
    36→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
    40→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
    41→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
    42→            continue
    43→
    44→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
    45→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
    46→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    47→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
    48→
    49→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
    50→        if impact:
    51→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
    52→        if remediation:
    53→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
    54→        warnings.append(line)
    55→    return warnings
    56→
    57→
    58→def show_post_scan_analysis(
    59→    diff: dict,
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    lang,
    62→    *,
    63→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
    64→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    65→    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    66→    # Critical warnings only (reopened, cascading, chronic, coverage reduction)
    67→    warnings: list[str] = []
    68→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
    69→        warnings.append(
    70→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?"
    71→        )
    72→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
    73→        warnings.append(
    74→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
    75→        )
    76→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
    77→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
    78→    if chronic_count > 0:
    79→        warnings.append(
    80→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — "
    81→            "run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
    82→        )
    83→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
    84→
    85→    for warning in warnings:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
    87→    if warnings:
    88→        print()
    89→
    90→    # Single narrative headline
    91→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    92→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    93→        state,
    94→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
    95→            diff=diff,
    96→            lang=lang_name,
    97→            command="scan",
    98→        ),
    99→    )
   100→    if narrative.get("headline"):

def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
    return state.get("overall_score")


def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
    return state.get("objective_score")


def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
    return state.get("strict_score")


def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.

    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
    """
```

   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState) -> str:
   292→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
   293→    findings = state["findings"]
   294→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   295→
   296→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   297→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   298→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   299→    lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   300→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   301→
   302→    return "\n".join(lines)
   303→
```

    14→    threshold_label: str

## Comprehensive Analysis Report

Based on my thorough investigation of the codebase, here are the findings for each area you requested:

### 1. **Plan Command Rendering** (`plan_cmd.py` + `render.py`)

**Files:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan_cmd.py` (lines 1-38)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/render.py` (lines 1-303)

**How it works:**
The `cmd_plan_output()` function (plan_cmd.py:16) orchestrates the plan generation by:
1. Getting the state via `command_runtime()`
2. Calling `planning_mod.generate_plan_md()` to render the entire plan as markdown
3. Either writing to a file (if `--output` flag provided) or printing to stdout

**Output structure** (render.py:291-302):
The `generate_plan_md()` function assembles the plan in this order:
1. **Header** (render.py:17-64): Title, three-score summary (overall/objective/strict), codebase metrics (files/LOC/dirs)
2. **Dimension Health Table** (render.py:67-169): Multi-row table showing each dimension with Checks/Issues/Health%/Strict%/Action columns. Separates objective dimensions from subjective measures.
3. **Tier Summary** (render.py:246-260): 4-line summary of each tier with open/total/addressed counts
4. **Per-Tier Sections** (render.py:172-243): Detailed findings grouped by tier (T1-T4), then by file, with confidence badges
5. **Addressed Section** (render.py:263-288): Summary of fixed/wontfix/auto-resolved findings + wontfix explanations (limited to 30)

**Typical output length:**
- Short/new projects: ~200-300 lines
- Medium projects: ~500-1000 lines (with T3/T4 structural decomposition visible)
- Large projects: 1500+ lines (all tiers populated)

**Key insight:** The tier labels are critical:
- **T1**: "Auto-fixable (imports, logs, dead deprecated)"
- **T2**: "Quick fixes (unused vars, dead exports, exact dupes, orphaned files, cross-tool imports)"
- **T3**: "Needs judgment (smells, near-dupes, single-use, small cycles, state sync)"
- **T4**: "Major refactors (structural decomposition, large import cycles)"
(Source: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/common.py` lines 5-10)

### 4. **Narrative Headline Computation**

**File:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/headline.py` (lines 1-158)
- Orchestrated from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/core.py` (lines 313-386)

**Key entry point:**
`compute_narrative()` (core.py:313-386) computes the headline via `_compute_headline()` (headline.py:8-48), which considers:

1. **Security prefix** (headline.py:22-26): ⚠ warning if `security` detector has open findings
2. **Review suffix** (headline.py:28-39): Note on uninvestigated vs pending review findings
3. **Base headline** (headline.py:51-157) via `_compute_headline_inner()`:
   - **Milestone override** (line 64): If a custom milestone exists, use it
   - **First scan** (lines 68-73): Celebrate completion + report finding count
   - **Regression** (lines 76-84): Acknowledge score drop after fixes
   - **Stagnation** (lines 87-113): Suggest focus dimension when stuck for 5+ scans
   - **Leverage point** (lines 116-122): Show lowest dimension + potential score impact
   - **Gap callout** (lines 124-132): Surface wontfix debt gap (overall vs strict)
   - **Maintenance** (lines 135-138): Announce maintenance mode
   - **Middle grind** (lines 141-150): Point to next action
   - **Early momentum** (lines 153-155): Celebrate trajectory

**Inputs used by `compute_narrative()`** (core.py:313-386):
- `state`: Full state dict including dimension_scores, findings, stats, scan_history
- `context.lang`: Language name (optional, scopes narrative to lang-specific history)
- `context.command`: Command name (scan, status, plan)
- `context.diff`: Diff payload (new, resolved, reopened counts)

**Secondary inputs used by headline:**
- `dimensions`: Lowest dimensions + gap dimensions + stagnant (computed by `_analyze_dimensions()`)
- `debt`: Wontfix/gap analysis (computed by `_analyze_debt()`)
- `phase`: Detected phase (first_scan, regression, stagnation, early_momentum, middle_grind, maintenance)
- `open_by_detector`: Counts per detector (security, review, etc.) — computed by `_count_open_by_detector()` (core.py:111-137)

3. **Metrics** (line 98): Files/LOC/dirs + last scan time
4. **Open scope breakdown** (line 99): In-scope vs out-of-scope open counts
5. **Scan completeness** (line 100): Warn if partial scan (e.g., slow phases skipped)
6. **Dimension table** (line 103): If dim_scores exist — full health table with progress bars
7. **Dimension deltas** (lines 104-107): Show score model breakdown + detector contributions
8. **Focus suggestion** (line 112): Which lowest dimension to tackle + impact estimate
9. **Subjective followup** (lines 113-117): Concrete re-review commands for stale dimensions
10. **Review summary** (line 119): Count of open/uninvestigated review findings
11. **Structural debt by area** (line 120): Grouped T3/T4 debt weighted by area
12. **Agent plan** (line 121): Top action + narrative actions list
13. **Headline** (lines 123-125): Narrative headline (cyan)
14. **Ignore summary** (lines 127-128): Suppression patterns + % hidden findings
15. **Review staleness config** (lines 130-133): Custom review max age

### 1. **SCORE EXPLANATION** — Currently No Dedicated Glossary
**Status: Missing**

There is **no existing code that explains what the four scores mean** in user-facing help text or glossaries.

**What exists:**
- **Score definitions (code-only)** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` (lines 1 docstring):
  - `overall`: Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)
  - `objective`: Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)
  - `strict`: Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)
  - `verified`: Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes (None if not run yet)

- **Score display** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_summary.py` lines 59-101 (`show_score_delta`):
  - Prints: `overall {new.overall:.1f}/100  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100`
  - No explanation of what each means

- **LLM summary in scan output** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py` lines 228-278):
  - Has an "INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM" block telling agents to show ALL four scores
  - But does NOT explain what they mean

**Gap:** Users/agents see four scores with no legend explaining:
- What "overall" vs "objective" means (lenient vs mechanical)
- What "strict" vs "verified" means (unverified vs verified fixes)
- Why there are gaps between them

### 6. **CHRONIC REOPENER LOGIC** — Counted, Warned, Not Inflated
**Locations & behavior:**

**Definition** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` line 46):
```python
finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
```
A finding is "chronic reopener" when reopened **2+ times**.

**Tracking:**
- Incremented in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_findings.py` line 184 when finding reopens
- Stored in finding dict as `reopen_count` field

**Collection:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py` lines 197-200:
```python
chronic_reopeners = [
    finding.get("id")
    for finding in new_findings
    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
]
```

**Output:**
- Scan output warning: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_analysis.py` lines 76-82:
```
⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener(s) — run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them.
```
- Show output: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/render.py` lines 71-77:
```
⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix
```

**Key finding:** **NOT inflated by "never reviewed" files**
- Only counts findings that have been reopened 2+ times in the open state
- Does NOT include files that were never reviewed in the first place
- Pure reopening counter; distinct from subjective_review findings

Kept: critical warnings, single narrative headline, two pointers, `show_score_integrity()` for status. Changed import from `desloppify.utils` to `desloppify.core.output_api` for `colorize`.
     ```python
     def show_post_scan_analysis(diff, state, lang, *, target_strict_score=95.0):
         """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
         warnings: list[str] = []
         if diff["reopened"] > 5:
             warnings.append(f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?")
         if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
             warnings.append(f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading.")
         chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
         chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
         if chronic_count > 0:
             warnings.append(f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them.")
         warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
         for warning in warnings:
             print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
         if warnings:
             print()
         lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
         narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(state, context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(diff=diff, lang=lang_name, command="scan"))
         if narrative.get("headline"):
             print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
         print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item.", "dim"))
         print(colorize("  Run `desloppify status` for the full dashboard.", "dim"))
         print()
         return warnings, narrative

     def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:

             print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))

             print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))

             print(colorize(f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.", "dim"))
     ```

1→"""State scoring, statistics, and suppression accounting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from copy import deepcopy
     6→

     8→    "suppression_metrics",

    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,
    81→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    83→    }
    84→
    85→
    86→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    target: float,
    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    92→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    93→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    94→        subjective_assessments,
    95→        target=normalized_target,
    96→    )
    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
    98→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   100→
   101→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   102→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   103→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   104→
   105→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   106→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   107→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   108→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→            payload["score"] = 0.0

   113→                "score": 0.0,
   114→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",

   116→
   117→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   118→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions

   223→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   224→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   226→            impacts.append(
   227→                {
   228→                    "detector": str(detector_name),
   229→                    "status": status,
   230→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   231→                    "summary": summary,
   232→                }
   233→            )
   234→
   235→        if not impacts:
   236→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   237→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   238→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   239→            continue
   240→
   241→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   242→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   243→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(
   244→            min(coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0) for item in impacts),
   245→            2,
   246→        )
   247→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts
   248→
   249→    if not score_confidence_detectors:
   250→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   251→            "status": "full",
   252→            "confidence": 1.0,
   253→            "detectors": [],
   254→            "dimensions": [],
   255→        }
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    state["score_confidence"] = {
   259→        "status": "reduced",
   260→        "confidence": round(
   261→            min(
   262→                coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   263→                for item in score_confidence_detectors
   264→            ),
   265→            2,
   266→        ),
   267→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,
   268→        "dimensions": sorted(set(reduced_dimensions)),
   269→    }
   270→
   271→
   272→def _update_objective_health(
   273→    state: StateModel,
   274→    findings: dict,
   275→    *,
   276→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   277→) -> None:
   278→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   279→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   280→    if not pots:
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   284→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   285→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   286→        compute_health_score,
   287→        compute_score_bundle,
   288→        merge_potentials,
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   292→    if not merged:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   296→    integrity_target = (
   297→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   298→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   299→        else None
   300→    )
   301→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   302→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   303→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   304→            subjective_assessments,
   305→            target=integrity_target,
   306→        )
   307→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   308→
   309→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   310→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   311→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   312→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   313→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   314→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   316→        return
   317→
   318→    # Derive language-scoped subjective dimensions so only dimensions with
   319→    # a review pathway get 0-score placeholders.  Explicit assessments
   320→    # outside this set still count (append_subjective_dimensions handles
   321→    # that).  Without this, all 20 global default dimensions would get
   322→    # placeholders — including ones the language's review never covers.
   323→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   324→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   325→    if lang_name:
   326→        try:
   327→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.lang import (
   328→                HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG,
   329→            )
   330→
   331→            lang_dims = HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG.get(lang_name)
   332→            if lang_dims:
   333→                allowed_subjective = set(lang_dims)
   334→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   335→            pass
   336→
   337→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   338→        findings,
   339→        merged,
   340→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   341→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   342→    )
   343→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   344→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   345→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   346→
   347→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   348→
   349→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   350→        name: dict(
   351→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   352→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   355→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   356→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   357→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   358→        )
   359→        for name in lenient_scores
   360→    }
   361→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   362→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   363→
   364→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   365→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   366→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   367→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   368→            continue
   369→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   370→            continue
   371→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   372→            continue
   373→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   374→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   375→        carried.setdefault(
   376→            "verified_strict_score",
   377→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   378→        )
   379→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   380→
   381→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   382→        state,
   383→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   387→
   388→
   389→def _recompute_stats(
   390→    state: StateModel,
   391→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   392→    *,
   393→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   396→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   397→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   398→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   399→    state["stats"] = {
   400→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   401→        **counters,
   402→        "by_tier": {
   403→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   404→        },
   405→    }
   406→    _update_objective_health(
   407→        state,
   408→        findings,
   409→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   410→    )
   411→
   412→
   413→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   414→    return {
   415→        "last_ignored": 0,
   416→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   417→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   418→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   419→        "recent_scans": 0,
   420→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   421→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   422→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   423→    }
   424→
   425→
   426→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   427→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   428→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   429→    if not history:
   430→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   431→
   432→    scans_with_suppression = [
   433→        entry
   434→        for entry in history
   435→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   436→        and (
   437→            "ignored" in entry
   438→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   439→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   440→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   441→        )
   442→    ]
   443→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   444→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   445→
   446→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   447→    last = recent[-1]
   448→
   449→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   450→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   451→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   452→
   453→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   454→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   455→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   456→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   457→    else:
   458→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   459→
   460→    return {
   461→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   462→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   463→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   464→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   465→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   466→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   467→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   468→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   469→    }
   470→

1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    11→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    12→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    17→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    18→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    19→
    20→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    21→        return
    22→
    23→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    24→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    25→
    26→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    27→    if output:
    28→        try:
    29→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    30→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    31→            print_agent_plan(["Inspect and execute the generated plan."], next_command=next_command)
    32→        except OSError as e:
    33→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    34→    else:
    35→        print(plan_md)
    36→        print()
    37→        print_agent_plan(["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."], next_command=next_command)
    38→

    56→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:

    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""

    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),

    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:

    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))

    77→        print(

    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "

    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))

   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    try:
   111→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   112→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   113→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   116→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   117→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   124→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   125→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   128→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   129→        print(
   130→            colorize(
   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→
   151→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   152→
   153→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   154→    show_score_delta(
   155→        runtime.state,
   156→        merge.prev_overall,
   157→        merge.prev_objective,
   158→        merge.prev_strict,
   159→        merge.prev_verified,
   160→    )
   161→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   162→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   163→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   164→
   165→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   166→
   167→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   168→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:

   170→
   171→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(

   178→
   179→    write_query(

   190→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   191→    )

   196→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.

   198→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
