    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→
    89→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    90→
    91→    queue = build_work_queue(
    92→        state,
    93→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    94→            tier=tier,
    95→            count=count,
    96→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    97→            scope=scope,
    98→            status=status,
    99→            include_subjective=True,
   100→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   101→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   102→            explain=explain,
   103→        ),
   104→    )
   105→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   106→
   107→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   108→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   109→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   110→        state,
   111→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   115→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative
   116→    )
   117→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   118→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   119→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   120→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   121→        state,
   122→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   123→    )
   124→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   125→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   126→    ]
   127→    write_query(payload)
   128→
   129→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   130→    if output_file:
   131→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   132→            output_file,
   133→            payload,
   134→            len(items),
   135→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   136→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   137→        ):
   138→            return
   139→        raise SystemExit(1)
   140→
   141→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   145→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   150→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   151→        state.get("findings", {}),
   152→        state.get("scan_path"),
   153→    )
   154→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   155→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain
   156→    )
   157→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   158→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   159→        state,
   160→        dim_scores,
   161→        findings_scoped,
   162→        strict_score=strict_score,
   163→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   164→    )
   165→    print()
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   169→

    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)

    23→    if (

    26→        and strict_score is not None

    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState) -> str:
   292→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
   293→    findings = state["findings"]
   294→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   295→
   296→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   297→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   298→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   299→    lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   300→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   301→
   302→    return "\n".join(lines)
   303→

    51→def scorecard_dimension_entries(
    52→    state: dict,
    53→    *,
    54→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> list[dict]:
    56→    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""
    57→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    58→    subjective_by_name = {
    59→        entry["name"]: entry
    60→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(
    61→            state,
    62→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    63→        )
    64→    }
    65→    entries: list[dict] = []
    66→    for name, data in rows:
    67→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    68→        subjective_meta = subjective_by_name.get(name)
    69→        is_subjective = subjective_meta is not None
    70→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    71→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    72→        issues = int(data.get("issues", 0))
    73→        checks = int(data.get("checks", 0))
    74→        placeholder = bool(subjective_meta.get("placeholder")) if subjective_meta else False
    75→        not_scanned = bool(
    76→            not is_subjective and not detectors and checks == 0
    77→        )
    78→        carried_forward = bool(
    79→            not is_subjective and data.get("carried_forward")
    80→        )
    81→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    82→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False
    83→        cli_keys = (

   107→def show_scorecard_subjective_measures(state: dict) -> None:
   108→    """Show canonical scorecard dimensions only (mechanical + subjective)."""
   109→    entries = scorecard_dimension_entries(state)

   112→
   113→    print(colorize("  Scorecard dimensions (matches scorecard.png):", "dim"))
   114→    for entry in entries:

   120→            )
   121→            continue
   122→        bar = _dimension_bar(entry["score"])

   125→            suffix = colorize("  ⟲ prior scan", "dim")
   126→        elif entry.get("placeholder"):
   127→            suffix = colorize("  [unassessed]", "yellow")
   128→        elif entry.get("stale"):
   129→            suffix = colorize("  [stale — re-review]", "yellow")
   130→        print(
   131→            "  "
   132→            + f"{entry['name']:<18} {bar} {entry['score']:5.1f}%  "
   133→            + colorize(f"(strict {entry['strict']:5.1f}%)", "dim")
   134→            + suffix
   135→        )
   136→    stale_keys = [e["dimension_key"] for e in entries if e.get("stale")]
   137→    if stale_keys:
   138→        n = len(stale_keys)
   139→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   140→        print(
   141→            colorize(
   142→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   143→                f" — run `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
   144→                "yellow",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    print()
   148→
   149→
   150→def show_score_model_breakdown(state: dict, *, dim_scores: dict | None = None) -> None:
   151→    """Show score recipe and weighted drags so users can see what drives the north star."""
   152→    return presentation_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   153→        state,
   154→        scoring_mod=scoring_mod,
   155→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   156→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   157→    )
   158→
   159→
   160→def show_dimension_deltas(prev: dict, current: dict):
   161→    """Show which dimensions changed between scans (health and strict)."""
   162→    return presentation_mod.show_dimension_deltas(
   163→        prev,
   164→        current,
   165→        scoring_mod=scoring_mod,
   166→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   167→    )
   168→
   169→
   170→def show_low_dimension_hints(dim_scores: dict):
   171→    """Show actionable hints for dimensions below 50%."""
   172→    return presentation_mod.show_low_dimension_hints(
   173→        dim_scores,
   174→        scoring_mod=scoring_mod,
   175→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→def show_subjective_paths_section(
   180→    state: dict,
   181→    dim_scores: dict,
   182→    *,
   183→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   184→) -> None:
   185→    """Show explicit subjective-score improvement paths (coverage vs quality)."""
   186→    return show_subjective_paths(

   189→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   190→        scorecard_subjective_entries_fn=scorecard_subjective_entries,
   191→        threshold=threshold,
   192→    )

1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    10→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
    11→    DIMENSIONS,
    12→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
    13→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    14→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    15→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    16→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    17→    SCORING_MODES,
    18→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    19→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    20→    Dimension,
    21→    ScoreMode,
    22→)

    24→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float
    37→
    38→
    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(
   132→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
   140→    subjective_meta = (
   141→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
   142→        if isinstance(data, dict)
   143→        else {}
   144→    )
   145→    configured = (
   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
   147→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
   148→        else None
   149→    )
   150→    if is_numeric(configured):
   151→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
   152→
   153→    return float(
   154→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   155→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   156→            1.0,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)

1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    85→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    86→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    87→    )
    88→
    89→
    90→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    91→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    92→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    93→    supported: set[str] | None,
    94→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    95→    state: StateModel,
    96→) -> None:
    97→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    98→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    99→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   100→            continue
   101→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   102→        if count == 0:
   103→            continue
   104→
   105→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   106→        available_fixers = [
   107→            fixer
   108→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   109→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   110→            and _fixer_has_applicable_findings(state, detector, fixer)
   111→        ]
   112→        if not available_fixers:
   113→            actions.append(
   114→                {
   115→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   116→                    "detector": detector,
   117→                    "count": count,
   118→                    "description": (
   119→                        f"{count} {detector} findings — inspect with "
   120→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   121→                    ),
   122→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   123→                    "impact": impact,
   124→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   125→                }
   126→            )
   127→            continue
   128→
   129→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   130→        actions.append(
   131→            {
   132→                "type": "auto_fix",
   133→                "detector": detector,
   134→                "count": count,
   135→                "description": (
   136→                    f"{count} {detector} findings — run "
   137→                    f"`desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   138→                ),
   139→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   140→                "impact": impact,
   141→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   142→            }
   143→        )
   144→
   145→
   146→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   147→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   148→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   149→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   150→) -> None:

   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→

   291→
   292→def compute_health_score(
   293→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   294→) -> float:
   295→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   296→    return float(
   297→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   298→            "overall_score"
   299→        ]
   300→    )
   301→
   302→
   303→def compute_score_bundle(
   304→    findings: dict,
   305→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   306→    *,
   307→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   308→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   309→) -> ScoreBundle:
   310→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   311→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   312→        findings,
   313→        potentials,
   314→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   315→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   316→    )
   317→
   318→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   319→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   320→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   321→
   322→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   323→        name: data
   324→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   325→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    return ScoreBundle(
   329→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   330→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   331→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   332→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   333→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   334→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   335→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   336→    )
   337→
   338→
   339→def compute_score_impact(
   340→    dimension_scores: dict,
   341→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   342→    detector: str,
   343→    issues_to_fix: int,
   344→) -> float:
   345→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
   346→    target_dim = None
   347→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   348→        if detector in dim.detectors:
   349→            target_dim = dim
   350→            break
   351→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
   352→        return 0.0
   353→
   354→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
   355→    if potential <= 0:
   356→        return 0.0
   357→
   358→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
   359→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
   360→
   361→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
   362→    if not det_data:
   363→        return 0.0
   364→
   365→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
   366→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
   367→
   368→    total_potential = 0
   369→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
   370→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
   371→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
   372→        if not det_values:
   373→            continue
   374→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
   375→        if det == detector:
   376→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
   377→        else:
   378→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
   379→    if total_potential <= 0:
   380→        return 0.0
   381→
   382→    new_dim_score = (
   383→        max(
   384→            0.0,
   385→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
   386→        )
   387→        * 100
   388→    )
   389→
   390→    simulated = {k: dict(v) for k, v in dimension_scores.items()}
   391→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
   392→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
   393→
   394→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
   398→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
   399→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
   400→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
   401→        return None
   402→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ScoreBundle",
   407→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   408→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   409→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   410→    "compute_health_score",
   411→    "compute_score_bundle",
   412→    "compute_score_impact",
   413→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   414→]
   415→

    40→        )
    41→    return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "yellow") + colorize_fn(
    42→        "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    43→    )

   117→
   118→def show_dimension_deltas(
   119→    prev: dict,
   120→    current: dict,
   121→    *,
   122→    scoring_mod,
   123→    colorize_fn,
   124→) -> None:
   125→    """Show which dimensions changed between scans (health and strict)."""
   126→    moved = []
   127→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   128→        p = prev.get(dim.name, {})
   129→        n = current.get(dim.name, {})
   130→        if not p or not n:
   131→            continue
   132→        old_score = p.get("score", 100)
   133→        new_score = n.get("score", 100)
   134→        old_strict = p.get("strict", old_score)
   135→        new_strict = n.get("strict", new_score)
   136→        delta = new_score - old_score
   137→        strict_delta = new_strict - old_strict
   138→        if abs(delta) >= 0.1 or abs(strict_delta) >= 0.1:
   139→            moved.append(
   140→                (
   141→                    dim.name,
   142→                    old_score,
   143→                    new_score,
   144→                    delta,
   145→                    old_strict,
   146→                    new_strict,
   147→                    strict_delta,
   148→                )
   149→            )
   150→
   151→    if not moved:
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    print(colorize_fn("  Moved:", "dim"))
   155→    for name, old, new, delta, old_s, new_s, s_delta in sorted(
   156→        moved, key=lambda item: item[3]
   157→    ):
   158→        sign = "+" if delta > 0 else ""
   159→        color = "green" if delta > 0 else "red"
   160→        strict_str = ""
   161→        if abs(s_delta) >= 0.1:
   162→            s_sign = "+" if s_delta > 0 else ""
   163→            strict_str = colorize_fn(

   165→                "dim",
   166→            )
   167→        print(
   168→            colorize_fn(
   169→                f"    {name:<22} {old:.1f}% → {new:.1f}%  ({sign}{delta:.1f}%)", color
   170→            )
   171→            + strict_str
   172→        )
   173→    print()
   174→

   219→def show_detector_progress(
   220→    state: dict,
   221→    *,
   222→    state_mod: _StateMod,
   223→    narrative_mod: _NarrativeMod,
   224→    registry_mod: _RegistryMod,

   231→
   232→    by_detector: dict[str, dict] = {}
   233→    for finding in findings.values():
   234→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   235→        if detector in narrative_mod.STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   236→            detector = "structural"
   237→        if detector not in by_detector:
   238→            by_detector[detector] = {"open": 0, "total": 0}
   239→        by_detector[detector]["total"] += 1
   240→        if finding["status"] == "open":
   241→            by_detector[detector]["open"] += 1
   242→
   243→    detector_order = [
   244→        registry_mod.DETECTORS[d].display
   245→        for d in registry_mod.display_order()
   246→        if d in registry_mod.DETECTORS
   247→    ]
   248→    order_map = {display: i for i, display in enumerate(detector_order)}
   249→    sorted_dets = sorted(by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: order_map.get(item[0], 99))
   250→
   251→    print(colorize_fn("  Detector progress (open findings by detector):", "dim"))
   252→    print(colorize_fn("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   253→    bar_len = 15
   254→    for detector, data in sorted_dets:
   255→        total = data["total"]
   256→        open_count = data["open"]
   257→        addressed = total - open_count
   258→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   259→
   260→        filled = round(pct / 100 * bar_len)
   261→        if pct == 100:
   262→            bar = colorize_fn("█" * bar_len, "green")
   263→        elif open_count <= 2:
   264→            bar = colorize_fn("█" * filled, "green") + colorize_fn(
   265→                "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
   266→            )
   267→        else:
   268→            bar = colorize_fn("█" * filled, "yellow") + colorize_fn(
   269→                "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
   270→            )
   271→
   272→        det_label = detector.replace("_", " ").ljust(18)
   273→        open_str = (
   274→            colorize_fn(f"{open_count:3d} open", "yellow")
   275→            if open_count > 0
   276→            else colorize_fn("  ✓", "green")
   277→        )
   278→        print(
   279→            f"  {det_label} {bar} {pct:3d}%  {open_str}  {colorize_fn(f'/ {total}', 'dim')}"
   280→        )

   282→    print()

1→"""Dimension analysis and debt computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import STRUCTURAL_MERGE
     6→from desloppify.scoring import (
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    TIER_WEIGHTS,
     9→    compute_score_impact,
    10→    merge_potentials,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.state import StateModel, path_scoped_findings
    13→
    14→
    15→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:
    16→    """Compute per-dimension structured analysis."""
    17→    if not dim_scores:
    18→        return {}
    19→
    20→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    21→    return {
    22→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    23→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],
    24→        "stagnant_dimensions": _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores, history),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    30→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    31→        (
    32→            (name, ds)
    33→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    34→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    35→        ),
    36→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
    37→    )
    38→
    39→    lowest = []
    40→    for name, ds in sorted_dims[:3]:
    41→        strict = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    42→        issues = ds["issues"]
    43→        impact = _dominant_detector_impact(
    44→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    45→            detectors=ds.get("detectors", {}),
    46→            potentials=potentials,
    47→        )
    48→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in ds.get("detectors", {})
    49→        entry = {
    50→            "name": name,
    51→            "strict": round(strict, 1),
    52→            "issues": issues,
    53→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    54→        }
    55→        if is_subjective:
    56→            entry["subjective"] = True
    57→            entry["impact_description"] = "re-review to improve"
    58→        lowest.append(entry)
    59→    return lowest
    60→
    61→
    62→def _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Build summary entries for dimensions with the biggest strict gap."""
    64→    biggest_gap = []
    65→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(
    66→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    67→    )
    68→    for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
    69→        lenient = ds["score"]
    70→        strict = ds.get("strict", lenient)
    71→        gap = lenient - strict
    72→        if gap > 1.0:
    73→            wontfix_count = sum(
    74→                1
    75→                for f in scoped.values()
    76→                if f["status"] == "wontfix"
    77→                and _finding_in_dimension(f, name, dim_scores)
    78→            )
    79→            biggest_gap.append(
    80→                {
    81→                    "name": name,
    82→                    "lenient": round(lenient, 1),
    83→                    "strict": round(strict, 1),
    84→                    "gap": round(gap, 1),
    85→                    "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
    86→                }
    87→            )
    88→    biggest_gap.sort(key=lambda x: -x["gap"])
    89→    return biggest_gap
    90→
    91→
    92→def _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    93→    """Build summary entries for dimensions that have not moved recently."""
    94→    if len(history) < 3:
    95→        return []
    96→
    97→    stagnant = []
    98→    for name in dim_scores:
    99→        scores = _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name, history)
   100→        if len(scores) < 3 or max(scores) - min(scores) > 0.5:
   101→            continue
   102→        stagnant.append(
   103→            {
   104→                "name": name,
   105→                "strict": round(dim_scores[name].get("strict", dim_scores[name]["score"]), 1),
   106→                "stuck_scans": len(scores),
   107→            }
   108→        )
   109→    return stagnant
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name: str, history: list[dict]) -> list[float]:
   113→    """Collect recent strict score samples for one dimension."""
   114→    scores: list[float] = []
   115→    for history_entry in history[-5:]:
   116→        dim_entry = (history_entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(name)
   117→        if not dim_entry:
   118→            continue
   119→        strict_value = dim_entry.get("strict", dim_entry.get("score"))
   120→        if strict_value is not None:
   121→            scores.append(float(strict_value))
   122→    return scores
   123→
   124→
   125→def _dominant_detector_impact(
   126→    *,
   127→    dim_scores: dict,
   128→    detectors: dict,
   129→    potentials: dict,
   130→) -> float:
   131→    """Estimate impact using the most consequential detector in a dimension."""
   132→    normalized_scores = {
   133→        key: {
   134→            "score": value["score"],
   135→            "tier": value.get("tier", 3),
   136→            "detectors": value.get("detectors", {}),
   137→        }
   138→        for key, value in dim_scores.items()
   139→    }
   140→    impact = 0.0
   141→    for detector_name, detector_data in detectors.items():
   142→        issue_count = int(detector_data.get("issues", 0) or 0)
   143→        if issue_count <= 0:
   144→            continue
   145→        detector_impact = compute_score_impact(
   146→            normalized_scores,
   147→            potentials,
   148→            detector_name,
   149→            issue_count,
   150→        )

1→"""Planning public API with lazy imports to avoid import cycles."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER, TIER_LABELS

### 1. `compute_score_impact()` - Scoring System

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/core.py` (lines 339-394)

**Function Signature:**
```python
def compute_score_impact(
    dimension_scores: dict,
    potentials: dict[str, int],
    detector: str,
    issues_to_fix: int,
) -> float:
```

**What it does:**
- Estimates the overall health score improvement from fixing N issues in a specific detector
- Takes dimension scores, potentials dict (per-detector check counts), detector name, and count of issues to fix
- Returns the estimated score delta (rounded to 1 decimal place)
- Uses `compute_health_score()` to simulate the new score after fixing issues
- Returns 0.0 if detector not found, dimension not present, or potential <= 0

**Algorithm:**
1. Finds the dimension containing the detector
2. Extracts old weighted failures from detector metadata
3. Calculates new weighted failures = max(0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix)
4. Recomputes dimension score with new weighted failures
5. Simulates full dimension_scores dict with new dimension score
6. Computes new overall health score and returns difference

**Called by:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/action_engine.py` (line 54-55) - in `_impact_calculator()` closure for impact estimation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/dimensions.py` (line 145-150) - in `_dominant_detector_impact()` to estimate per-dimension impact
- Tests in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/scoring/test_scoring.py`

**Re-exported from:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/scoring.py` (line 40) - the main facade module

### 4. `next` Command

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py` - `cmd_next()` function

**Key parameters:**
- `--count N` (default 1) - number of items to show
- `--tier T` - filter by tier
- `--scope SCOPE` - filter by scope
- `--status STATUS` (default "open")
- `--group GROUP` (default "item") - grouping strategy
- `--format FORMAT` (default "terminal") - output format
- `--explain` - show reasoning for ordering

**Flow (lines 79-165):**
1. Builds work queue with `build_work_queue(state, QueueBuildOptions(...))`
2. Includes subjective dimensions in queue with threshold (default target_strict_score)
3. Computes narrative for context
4. Builds query payload with all scores and scorecard dimensions
5. Writes JSON query output
6. If output file specified, writes formatted output
7. If terminal output:
   - Renders queue header with explain flag
   - Checks for empty queue
   - Renders terminal items grouped by specified group
   - Shows single-item resolution hints
   - Shows followup nudges (low subjective dimensions, etc.)

**Re-exports found:**
- `next_output_mod.build_query_payload()`
- `next_render_mod.render_*` functions
